@@\\n+uid://cnp5b6fcwou2y\"},{\"sha\":\"000c59c22f76016404ab65314f8fee3364321723\",\"filename\":\"addons/godot_ai/clients/_cli_strategy.gd\",\"status\":\"added\",\"additions\":499,\"deletions\":0,\"changes\":499,\"blob_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/blob/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_cli_strategy.gd\",\"raw_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/raw/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_cli_strategy.gd\",\"contents_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/contents/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_cli_strategy.gd?ref=3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318\",\"patch\":\"@@ -0,0 +1,499 @@\\n+@tool\\n+class_name McpCliStrategy\\n+extends RefCounted\\n+\\n+## Strategy for MCP clients that own their own state via a CLI (e.g.\\n+## `claude mcp add`). Reads `cli_register_template` / `cli_unregister_template`\\n+## / `cli_status_args` from the descriptor and substitutes `{name}` / `{url}`\\n+## tokens. Command-shape descriptors additionally use the whole-element launch\\n+## tokens `{command}` / `{args...}` (see `format_args`). No descriptor-supplied\\n+## Callables — see `_base.gd` for why.\\n+##\\n+## Every shell-out goes through `McpCliExec.run`, which wraps the call in a\\n+## wall-clock timeout. A hung CLI (e.g. `claude mcp list` under\\n+## inter-Claude-Code contention) gets killed at the budget instead of\\n+## locking up the caller forever — see issues #238 / #239.\\n+\\n+## The descriptor token resolved from `godot_ai/mcp_client_scope` (#872).\\n+## Only Claude Code takes it today; any `config_type = \\\"cli\\\"` descriptor can.\\n+const SCOPE_TOKEN := \\\"{scope}\\\"\\n+\\n+const _CONFIGURE_TIMEOUT_MS := 10000\\n+const _REMOVE_TIMEOUT_MS := 10000\\n+const _STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS := 6000\\n+const MutationLock := preload(\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/utils/client_mutation_lock.gd\\\")\\n+\\n+\\n+static func configure(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary = {},\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar write_error := configure_write_error(client)\\n+\\tif not write_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": write_error}\\n+\\t## Fail closed before any subprocess runs: a command-shape client without a\\n+\\t## verified attach launcher must not register anything (see\\n+\\t## docs/client-configuration.md — an ERROR beats an entry known to be broken).\\n+\\tvar launch_error := command_launch_error(client, launch)\\n+\\tif not launch_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": launch_error}\\n+\\tvar cli := _resolve_cli(client)\\n+\\tif cli.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"%s not found\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\treturn _configure_claimed(client, server_name, server_url, launch, cli)\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _configure_claimed(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary,\\n+\\tcli: String,\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\n+\\t# Best-effort prior cleanup so re-configure is idempotent. Ordinary absent-\\n+\\t# entry/non-zero results remain ignorable; an unproven timeout is not,\\n+\\t# because its descendant could race the configure that follows.\\n+\\tif not client.cli_unregister_template.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t## #872: a `{scope}` descriptor sweeps EVERY scope, not just the\\n+\\t\\t## selected one. Flipping the setting user -> project and pressing\\n+\\t\\t## Configure would otherwise leave the old user-scope entry alive —\\n+\\t\\t## exactly the \\\"loaded in every unrelated workspace\\\" problem the\\n+\\t\\t## setting exists to fix. `mcp remove` on an absent entry is a no-op,\\n+\\t\\t## so ordinary results are discarded; ambiguous termination aborts the\\n+\\t\\t## sequence. Each extra scope costs one bounded subprocess.\\n+\\t\\tfor pre_scope in _cleanup_scopes(client):\\n+\\t\\t\\tvar pre_args := _format_args(\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tclient.cli_unregister_template, server_name, server_url, {}, pre_scope\\n+\\t\\t\\t)\\n+\\t\\t\\tvar cleaned := McpCliExec.run(cli, pre_args, _REMOVE_TIMEOUT_MS)\\n+\\t\\t\\tvar cleanup_failure := _unproven_mutation_failure(\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tclient, \\\"Prior cleanup\\\", cleaned\\n+\\t\\t\\t)\\n+\\t\\t\\tif not cleanup_failure.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\treturn cleanup_failure\\n+\\n+\\tif client.cli_register_template.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"%s descriptor missing cli_register_template\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\tvar args := _format_args(client.cli_register_template, server_name, server_url, launch)\\n+\\tvar result := McpCliExec.run(cli, args, _CONFIGURE_TIMEOUT_MS)\\n+\\tvar termination_failure := _unproven_mutation_failure(client, \\\"Configure\\\", result)\\n+\\tif not termination_failure.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn termination_failure\\n+\\tif result.get(\\\"timed_out\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"message\\\": \\\"Configure %s timed out after %ds — see 'Run this manually' below to retry by hand\\\" % [\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tclient.display_name, _CONFIGURE_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000,\\n+\\t\\t\\t],\\n+\\t\\t}\\n+\\tif result.get(\\\"spawn_failed\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Failed to spawn %s\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\tif int(result.get(\\\"exit_code\\\", -1)) == 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\", \\\"message\\\": McpClient.configured_message(client, server_url)}\\n+\\t## `claude mcp add` writes its real failure diagnostics to stderr, so\\n+\\t## prefer `output` (stdout + stderr) over `stdout` alone — otherwise\\n+\\t## the user sees \\\"exit code 1\\\" instead of the actual error.\\n+\\tvar combined := str(result.get(\\\"output\\\", \\\"\\\")).strip_edges()\\n+\\tvar err := combined if not combined.is_empty() else \\\"exit code %d\\\" % int(result.get(\\\"exit_code\\\", -1))\\n+\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Failed to configure %s: %s\\\" % [client.display_name, err]}\\n+\\n+\\n+## Run the descriptor's `cli_status_args`, scan stdout for `server_name` and\\n+## the expected target. The matching rule is the only sensible one for \\\"list\\n+## MCP entries\\\" output across CLI clients we currently support: name AND\\n+## target present → CONFIGURED; name only → MISMATCH; neither →\\n+## NOT_CONFIGURED. For URL descriptors the target is `server_url`; for\\n+## command-shape descriptors it is the resolved attach launcher path (the\\n+## listing prints the registered command line, not a URL). Command-shape CLI\\n+## clients with a JSON fallback file get exact drift detection via the JSON\\n+## strategy instead — the configurator prefers that path and only lands here\\n+## for CLI clients whose state isn't file-readable.\\n+static func check_status(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient, server_name: String, server_url: String, launch: Dictionary = {}\\n+) -> McpClient.Status:\\n+\\treturn check_status_with_cli_path(client, server_name, server_url, _resolve_cli(client), launch)\\n+\\n+\\n+static func check_status_with_cli_path(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient, server_name: String, server_url: String, cli: String, launch: Dictionary = {}\\n+) -> McpClient.Status:\\n+\\treturn check_status_details(client, server_name, server_url, cli, launch).get(\\\"status\\\", McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Detailed variant used by the dock's refresh worker so it can surface a\\n+## \\\"probe timed out\\\" badge on the affected row instead of silently\\n+## conflating the timeout with NOT_CONFIGURED. Returns\\n+## `{\\\"status\\\": Status, \\\"error_msg\\\": String}`. The caller plumbs\\n+## `error_msg` straight into `_apply_row_status`.\\n+static func check_status_details(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient, server_name: String, server_url: String, cli: String, launch: Dictionary = {}\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tif cli.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\tif client.cli_status_args.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\tvar expected_target := server_url\\n+\\tif client.command_shape != McpClient.CommandShape.NONE:\\n+\\t\\t## Same fail-closed contract as configure: without a verified launcher\\n+\\t\\t## there is no target to compare against, and guessing would report a\\n+\\t\\t## broken entry as green.\\n+\\t\\tvar launch_error := command_launch_error(client, launch)\\n+\\t\\tif not launch_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.ERROR, launch_error)\\n+\\t\\texpected_target = str(launch.get(\\\"command\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tvar result := McpCliExec.run(\\n+\\t\\tcli,\\n+\\t\\tMcpClient._array_from_packed(client.cli_status_args),\\n+\\t\\t_STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS,\\n+\\t\\tfalse\\n+\\t)\\n+\\tif result.get(\\\"timed_out\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.ERROR, \\\"probe timed out\\\")\\n+\\tif result.get(\\\"spawn_failed\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\tif int(result.get(\\\"exit_code\\\", -1)) != 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\tvar text := str(result.get(\\\"stdout\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tif text.find(server_name) < 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\t## Server registered, but pointing somewhere else — drift after a\\n+\\t## port change. Surface as mismatch so the dock offers Reconfigure.\\n+\\tif text.find(expected_target) < 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(\\n+\\t\\t\\tMcpClient.Status.CONFIGURED_MISMATCH, \\\"\\\", _probe_output_launches_godot_ai(text)\\n+\\t\\t)\\n+\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.CONFIGURED)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Empty string when this client's launch requirements are satisfied. A\\n+## command-shape descriptor requires a successfully resolved attach launch;\\n+## URL descriptors (`CommandShape.NONE`) never require one. Mirrors\\n+## `McpJsonStrategy.command_launch_error` / the TOML equivalent.\\n+static func command_launch_error(client: McpClient, launch: Dictionary) -> String:\\n+\\tif client.command_shape == McpClient.CommandShape.NONE:\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tif not bool(launch.get(\\\"ok\\\", false)):\\n+\\t\\treturn str(launch.get(\\\"error\\\", \\\"No compatible attach launcher was found.\\\"))\\n+\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _status_details(\\n+\\tstatus: McpClient.Status, error_msg: String = \\\"\\\", owned: bool = false\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": status, \\\"error_msg\\\": error_msg, \\\"owned\\\": owned}\\n+\\n+\\n+static func remove(client: McpClient, server_name: String) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar write_error := remove_write_error(client)\\n+\\tif not write_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": write_error}\\n+\\tvar cli := _resolve_cli(client)\\n+\\tif cli.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"%s not found\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\treturn _remove_claimed(client, server_name, cli)\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _remove_claimed(client: McpClient, server_name: String, cli: String) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tif client.cli_unregister_template.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"%s descriptor missing cli_unregister_template\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\tvar args := _format_args(client.cli_unregister_template, server_name, \\\"\\\")\\n+\\tvar result := McpCliExec.run(cli, args, _REMOVE_TIMEOUT_MS)\\n+\\tvar termination_failure := _unproven_mutation_failure(client, \\\"Remove\\\", result)\\n+\\tif not termination_failure.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn termination_failure\\n+\\tif result.get(\\\"timed_out\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"message\\\": \\\"Remove %s timed out after %ds — see 'Run this manually' below to retry by hand\\\" % [\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tclient.display_name, _REMOVE_TIMEOUT_MS / 1000,\\n+\\t\\t\\t],\\n+\\t\\t}\\n+\\tif result.get(\\\"spawn_failed\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Failed to spawn %s\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\tif int(result.get(\\\"exit_code\\\", -1)) == 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"%s configuration removed\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\t## `claude mcp add` writes its real failure diagnostics to stderr, so\\n+\\t## prefer `output` (stdout + stderr) over `stdout` alone — otherwise\\n+\\t## the user sees \\\"exit code 1\\\" instead of the actual error.\\n+\\tvar combined := str(result.get(\\\"output\\\", \\\"\\\")).strip_edges()\\n+\\tvar err := combined if not combined.is_empty() else \\\"exit code %d\\\" % int(result.get(\\\"exit_code\\\", -1))\\n+\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Failed to remove %s: %s\\\" % [client.display_name, err]}\\n+\\n+\\n+## A POSIX exact-PID kill cannot prove that a CLI's descendants stopped. Never\\n+## issue the next write after that ambiguous boundary: the old process could\\n+## otherwise land a stale mutation after the new one. The durable global claim\\n+## remains until the user explicitly clears it after stopping those processes.\\n+static func _unproven_mutation_failure(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient, operation: String, result: Dictionary\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tif not bool(result.get(\\\"termination_failed\\\", false)):\\n+\\t\\treturn {}\\n+\\treturn {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\\"message\\\": (\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"%s for %s could not be proven stopped. %s\\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t% [operation, client.display_name, MutationLock.recovery_message()]\\n+\\t\\t),\\n+\\t\\t\\\"termination_failed\\\": true,\\n+\\t}\\n+\\n+\\n+## Substitute `{name}` and `{url}` tokens in every template entry.\\n+## Tokens match verbatim — `{name_suffix}` is NOT touched, so callers don't\\n+## have to worry about partial-token collisions in their argv.\\n+##\\n+## Launch tokens are whole-element only: an element that is exactly\\n+## `{command}` becomes the resolved attach launcher path, and an element that\\n+## is exactly `{args...}` is spliced into the argv as one element per launch\\n+## arg. Whole-element matching keeps a literal brace inside a path or flag\\n+## from ever triggering an expansion.\\n+##\\n+## `scope_override` forces a specific `{scope}` value instead of the live\\n+## setting; the configure pre-cleanup uses it to sweep the scopes the user is\\n+## NOT currently on. Empty means \\\"resolve from the setting\\\".\\n+static func format_args(\\n+\\ttemplate: PackedStringArray,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary = {},\\n+\\tscope_override: String = \\\"\\\",\\n+) -> Array[String]:\\n+\\treturn _format_args(template, server_name, server_url, launch, scope_override)\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _format_args(\\n+\\ttemplate: PackedStringArray,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary = {},\\n+\\tscope_override: String = \\\"\\\",\\n+) -> Array[String]:\\n+\\tvar scope := scope_override if not scope_override.is_empty() else McpSettings.client_scope()\\n+\\tvar out: Array[String] = []\\n+\\tfor arg in template:\\n+\\t\\tvar s := String(arg)\\n+\\t\\tif s == \\\"{command}\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\tout.append(str(launch.get(\\\"command\\\", \\\"\\\")))\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tif s == \\\"{args...}\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\tfor launch_arg in launch.get(\\\"args\\\", []):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tout.append(str(launch_arg))\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\ts = s.replace(\\\"{name}\\\", server_name)\\n+\\t\\ts = s.replace(\\\"{url}\\\", server_url)\\n+\\t\\t## Resolved per-call rather than baked into the descriptor so the\\n+\\t\\t## setting can change without an editor restart, and so the manual\\n+\\t\\t## command shown in the dock always matches what Configure would run.\\n+\\t\\ts = s.replace(SCOPE_TOKEN, scope)\\n+\\t\\tout.append(s)\\n+\\treturn out\\n+\\n+\\n+## Scope-aware status for descriptors that declare `cli_scope_status_template`.\\n+## Returns the same `{\\\"status\\\", \\\"error_msg\\\"}` shape as check_status_details.\\n+##\\n+## Split from the subprocess call so the verdict is a pure function of what the\\n+## CLI printed — `_scope_probe_verdict` is unit-tested against real recorded\\n+## `claude mcp get` output rather than needing a `claude` binary on the runner.\\n+static func check_scope_status_details(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tcli: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary,\\n+\\texpected_scope: String,\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tif cli.is_empty() or client.cli_scope_status_template.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\tvar expected_target := server_url\\n+\\tvar expected_args: Array[String] = []\\n+\\tvar expected_type := \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tif client.command_shape != McpClient.CommandShape.NONE:\\n+\\t\\t## Same fail-closed contract as configure and the plain status probe.\\n+\\t\\tvar launch_error := command_launch_error(client, launch)\\n+\\t\\tif not launch_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.ERROR, launch_error)\\n+\\t\\texpected_target = str(launch.get(\\\"command\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\t\\tfor launch_arg in launch.get(\\\"args\\\", []):\\n+\\t\\t\\texpected_args.append(str(launch_arg))\\n+\\t\\texpected_type = str(client.command_transport_value)\\n+\\tvar args := _format_args(client.cli_scope_status_template, server_name, server_url)\\n+\\tvar result := McpCliExec.run(cli, args, _STATUS_TIMEOUT_MS, false)\\n+\\tif result.get(\\\"timed_out\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.ERROR, \\\"probe timed out\\\")\\n+\\tif result.get(\\\"spawn_failed\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\treturn _scope_probe_verdict(\\n+\\t\\tint(result.get(\\\"exit_code\\\", -1)),\\n+\\t\\tstr(result.get(\\\"stdout\\\", \\\"\\\")),\\n+\\t\\texpected_scope,\\n+\\t\\texpected_target,\\n+\\t\\texpected_args,\\n+\\t\\texpected_type,\\n+\\t)\\n+\\n+\\n+## The `Scope:` label the probe printed, normalised to a CLIENT_SCOPES value,\\n+## or \\\"\\\" when no line was recognisable. Matched on the first word after the\\n+## label so the parenthetical blurb (\\\"(shared via .mcp.json)\\\") can change\\n+## wording between CLI releases without breaking detection.\\n+static func _scope_from_probe_output(text: String) -> String:\\n+\\tfor raw_line in text.split(\\\"\\\\n\\\"):\\n+\\t\\tvar line := String(raw_line).strip_edges()\\n+\\t\\tvar marker := line.find(\\\"Scope:\\\")\\n+\\t\\tif marker < 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tvar rest := line.substr(marker + 6).strip_edges().to_lower()\\n+\\t\\tfor scope in McpSettings.CLIENT_SCOPES:\\n+\\t\\t\\tvar name := String(scope)\\n+\\t\\t\\tif rest.begins_with(name):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\treturn name\\n+\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+## The probe output names our server; only its launch fields decide whether\\n+## the existing entry is ours to rewrite after an update.\\n+static func _probe_output_launches_godot_ai(text: String) -> bool:\\n+\\treturn McpClient.launch_mentions_godot_ai(\\n+\\t\\t_field_from_probe_output(text, \\\"Command:\\\")\\n+\\t\\t+ \\\" \\\"\\n+\\t\\t+ _field_from_probe_output(text, \\\"Args:\\\")\\n+\\t\\t+ \\\" \\\"\\n+\\t\\t+ _field_from_probe_output(text, \\\"URL:\\\")\\n+\\t)\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _field_from_probe_output(text: String, label: String) -> String:\\n+\\tfor raw_line in text.split(\\\"\\\\n\\\"):\\n+\\t\\tvar line := String(raw_line).strip_edges()\\n+\\t\\tif line.begins_with(label):\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn line.substr(label.length()).strip_edges()\\n+\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+## Pure verdict for a scope probe. A non-zero exit is the CLI's \\\"no such\\n+## server\\\" signal. An entry resolved from a scope other than the selected one\\n+## is MISMATCH, not CONFIGURED: the dock offers Reconfigure, and Configure's\\n+## all-scope pre-cleanup is what actually moves it.\\n+##\\n+## A missing or unrecognised `Scope:` line degrades to the target check alone,\\n+## deliberately rather than failing closed. `_verify_post_state` turns any\\n+## non-CONFIGURED status after a successful write into an error, so treating an\\n+## unparsed label as MISMATCH would mean a future CLI release that reworded or\\n+## dropped that line breaks Configure outright — a false error plus a\\n+## permanently amber row whose Reconfigure button cannot clear it. The entry\\n+## has already been matched by name and exact launcher path at that point; the\\n+## only thing in doubt is which scope it came from, and a slightly optimistic\\n+## dot is a much cheaper failure than a configure flow that reports success as\\n+## failure. Today's claude (2.1.241) always prints the line, so this is a\\n+## forward-compatibility hedge, not a live code path.\\n+static func _scope_probe_verdict(\\n+\\texit_code: int,\\n+\\ttext: String,\\n+\\texpected_scope: String,\\n+\\texpected_target: String,\\n+\\texpected_args: Array[String] = [],\\n+\\texpected_type: String = \\\"\\\",\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tif exit_code != 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\t## `resolved_scope` rides along as structured data so callers can act on it\\n+\\t## — `_verify_post_state`'s path hint needs to know WHICH scope survived to\\n+\\t## name the right file, and parsing it back out of the human-facing message\\n+\\t## would couple that hint to this wording (#879). Parsed BEFORE the target\\n+\\t## check so both mismatch paths carry it: an entry whose launcher drifted\\n+\\t## sends the user to the wrong file just as readily as one whose scope did.\\n+\\tvar resolved := _scope_from_probe_output(text)\\n+\\tvar command_matches := (\\n+\\t\\texpected_target.is_empty()\\n+\\t\\tor _field_from_probe_output(text, \\\"Command:\\\") == expected_target\\n+\\t)\\n+\\tvar launch_details_match := true\\n+\\tif not expected_type.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tlaunch_details_match = (\\n+\\t\\t\\t_field_from_probe_output(text, \\\"Type:\\\") == expected_type\\n+\\t\\t\\tand _field_from_probe_output(text, \\\"Args:\\\") == \\\" \\\".join(expected_args)\\n+\\t\\t)\\n+\\tif not command_matches or not launch_details_match:\\n+\\t\\tvar drifted := _status_details(\\n+\\t\\t\\tMcpClient.Status.CONFIGURED_MISMATCH, \\\"\\\", _probe_output_launches_godot_ai(text)\\n+\\t\\t)\\n+\\t\\tif not resolved.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\tdrifted[\\\"resolved_scope\\\"] = resolved\\n+\\t\\treturn drifted\\n+\\tif resolved.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.CONFIGURED)\\n+\\tif resolved != expected_scope:\\n+\\t\\tvar details := _status_details(\\n+\\t\\t\\tMcpClient.Status.CONFIGURED_MISMATCH,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"registered at %s scope, not %s\\\" % [resolved, expected_scope],\\n+\\t\\t\\t_probe_output_launches_godot_ai(text),\\n+\\t\\t)\\n+\\t\\tdetails[\\\"resolved_scope\\\"] = resolved\\n+\\t\\treturn details\\n+\\treturn _status_details(McpClient.Status.CONFIGURED)\\n+\\n+\\n+## True when the descriptor's register template takes `{scope}`, i.e. where\\n+## its entry lands is decided by the `godot_ai/mcp_client_scope` setting rather\\n+## than fixed by the descriptor. The configurator uses this to decide whether\\n+## the JSON-fallback file is still a valid place to read status back from.\\n+static func uses_scope_token(client: McpClient) -> bool:\\n+\\treturn client.cli_register_template.has(SCOPE_TOKEN)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Why Configure may not run this client's CLI. The CLI runs inside this\\n+## editor's Flatpak sandbox, so what it puts in the file the client keeps its\\n+## servers in (the JSON-fallback file) lands where the client outside never\\n+## reads it. That file takes the `user` scope's entry and the `local` scope's\\n+## per-project block, and the pre-cleanup sweep (#872) removes from it at\\n+## every scope, so the selected scope makes no difference here: at `project`\\n+## the sweep would miss a stale entry in the host's file and Configure would\\n+## still report success. A descriptor that names no such file has nothing to\\n+## check.\\n+static func configure_write_error(client: McpClient) -> String:\\n+\\tif not client.has_json_fallback():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\treturn client.config_write_error()\\n+\\n+\\n+## Why Remove may not. It removes from the selected scope only: `user` and\\n+## `local` keep their entries in the JSON-fallback file, and `project` in the\\n+## CLI's working directory, which this layer does not resolve and leaves to\\n+## the CLI.\\n+static func remove_write_error(client: McpClient) -> String:\\n+\\tif not client.has_json_fallback():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tif uses_scope_token(client) and McpSettings.client_scope() == \\\"project\\\":\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\treturn client.config_write_error()\\n+\\n+\\n+## Public view of the pre-cleanup sweep, for the manual-command text: what the\\n+## dock tells the user to run has to match what Configure actually runs (#877).\\n+static func cleanup_scopes(client: McpClient) -> Array[String]:\\n+\\treturn _cleanup_scopes(client)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Scopes the configure pre-cleanup removes from. A descriptor without the\\n+## `{scope}` token has exactly one place its entry can live, so it keeps the\\n+## single pass it always had — \\\"\\\" means \\\"resolve the scope normally\\\".\\n+static func _cleanup_scopes(client: McpClient) -> Array[String]:\\n+\\tif not uses_scope_token(client):\\n+\\t\\treturn [\\\"\\\"]\\n+\\tvar scopes: Array[String] = []\\n+\\tfor scope in McpSettings.CLIENT_SCOPES:\\n+\\t\\tscopes.append(String(scope))\\n+\\treturn scopes\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _resolve_cli(client: McpClient) -> String:\\n+\\treturn McpCliFinder.find(McpClient._array_from_packed(client.cli_names))\\n+\\n+\\n+static func resolve_cli_path(client: McpClient) -> String:\\n+\\treturn _resolve_cli(client)\"},{\"sha\":\"f84a92b65ffa2ef9187265b9ae69a0a899903042\",\"filename\":\"addons/godot_ai/clients/_cli_strategy.gd.uid\",\"status\":\"added\",\"additions\":1,\"deletions\":0,\"changes\":1,\"blob_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/blob/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_cli_strategy.gd.uid\",\"raw_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/raw/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_cli_strategy.gd.uid\",\"contents_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/contents/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_cli_strategy.gd.uid?ref=3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318\",\"patch\":\"@@ -0,0 +1 @@\\n+uid://bvib7d8eabbcm\"},{\"sha\":\"5df8f6f3293004ddee30f33d7e2df8e9ff52ef07\",\"filename\":\"addons/godot_ai/clients/_dsh_strategy.gd\",\"status\":\"added\",\"additions\":771,\"deletions\":0,\"changes\":771,\"blob_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/blob/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_dsh_strategy.gd\",\"raw_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/raw/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_dsh_strategy.gd\",\"contents_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/contents/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_dsh_strategy.gd?ref=3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318\",\"patch\":\"@@ -0,0 +1,771 @@\\n+@tool\\n+class_name McpDshStrategy\\n+extends RefCounted\\n+\\n+## Configure / status / remove for DeepSeek Harness (dsh) MCP servers.\\n+##\\n+## DeepSeek Harness has no `mcp` CLI verb (verified against dsh\\n+## 0.1.0-rc.6 — `dsh --help` exposes only `web` and `plugin`). MCP servers\\n+## register as `@deepseek-ai/dsh-mcp-client` plugin entries in the HOME patch\\n+## layer `$DSH_HOME/cordis.patch.yml` — the machine-local layer dsh applies\\n+## over EVERY profile's own patch layer (the web GUI included), so one entry\\n+## covers every profile the user boots.\\n+##\\n+## The patch file is a top-level YAML array of loader patch entries\\n+## (`@deepseek-ai/cordis-plugin-include`'s `applyEntryPatches`). A non-insert\\n+## row only OVERRIDES an existing bundle row (a new id is skipped with a\\n+## warning), so a brand-new server must be added with an `insert` row:\\n+##\\n+##     - insert:\\n+##         - id: mcp-godot-ai\\n+##           name: '@deepseek-ai/dsh-mcp-client'\\n+##           config:\\n+##             serverName: godot-ai\\n+##             transport: stdio\\n+##             command: \\\"...\\\"\\n+##             args: [...]\\n+##\\n+## Both shapes verified live against the dsh loader\\n+## (`dsh --profile web --dump-config` composes the inserted entry; a plain\\n+## row for a new id warns \\\"patch: entry not found\\\" and is skipped). We write\\n+## one dedicated insert row per server, preserve every other row byte-for-byte\\n+## (including other users' insert rows, overrides, comments, and `!!js`\\n+## expressions), and status-verify the entry we wrote — the same file-read\\n+## drift contract the JSON/TOML/YAML strategies use.\\n+##\\n+## The entry's `config` nests the launch under `serverName`/`transport`/\\n+## `command`/`args`; `serverName` is the model-facing tool namespace\\n+## (`mcp__godot-ai__<tool>`), so it carries the server name like the\\n+## `server_key_path` map key does for the other strategies.\\n+\\n+## Loader entry id prefix inside the patch list. The full id is\\n+## `<prefix><server_name>` — \\\"mcp-godot-ai\\\" for the godot-ai server.\\n+const ENTRY_ID_PREFIX := \\\"mcp-\\\"\\n+## The loader plugin that bridges MCP servers into the harness. Must be\\n+## installed in the profile's node_modules (dsh ships it as a dependency of\\n+## the CLI package). Quoted in the file: `@` is a reserved YAML indicator and\\n+## cannot start a plain scalar.\\n+const PLUGIN_NAME := \\\"@deepseek-ai/dsh-mcp-client\\\"\\n+## Indent of a nested entry header under `- insert:` (children step 2 spaces\\n+## deeper), matching the shipped profile patch files.\\n+const ENTRY_INDENT := 4\\n+\\n+\\n+static func entry_id(server_name: String) -> String:\\n+\\treturn ENTRY_ID_PREFIX + server_name\\n+\\n+\\n+static func configure(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary = {},\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar resolution := client.resolved_config_path_details()\\n+\\tvar path := str(resolution.get(\\\"path\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tvar path_error := str(resolution.get(\\\"error\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tif path_error.is_empty() and not path.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tpath_error = McpClient.unshared_flatpak_config_error(client.display_name, path)\\n+\\tif not path_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": path_error}\\n+\\tif path.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Could not resolve config path for %s on this OS\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\t## Set only while no candidate file exists, so this never blocks an update.\\n+\\tvar create_error := str(resolution.get(\\\"create_error\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tif not create_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": create_error}\\n+\\t## Fail closed before touching the file — same contract as JSON/TOML/YAML.\\n+\\tvar launch_error := command_launch_error(client, launch)\\n+\\tif not launch_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": launch_error}\\n+\\n+\\tvar read := _read(path)\\n+\\tif not read[\\\"ok\\\"]:\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Refusing to overwrite %s: %s. Fix or move the file, then re-run Configure.\\\" % [path, read[\\\"error\\\"]]}\\n+\\t## Fail closed on content that is not a loader patch list: dsh's patch\\n+\\t## parser rejects a non-array file at boot, so appending our row to a\\n+\\t## top-level mapping would hand the user a broken file (#867 review).\\n+\\t## `[]` is a valid empty sequence meaning \\\"no rows\\\"; the literal must not\\n+\\t## survive an append (block rows after `[]` are malformed YAML), so only\\n+\\t## that line is dropped — comments around it belong to the user and stay.\\n+\\tvar text := String(read[\\\"data\\\"])\\n+\\ttext = _strip_empty_flow_sequence(text)\\n+\\tif not _is_patch_sequence(text):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"message\\\": \\\"Refusing to overwrite %s: it is not a top-level YAML list of loader entries. Fix or remove the file, then re-run Configure.\\\" % path,\\n+\\t\\t}\\n+\\n+\\tvar existing_config: Variant = null\\n+\\tvar lines := _split_lines(text)\\n+\\tvar found := _find_entry_block(lines, entry_id(server_name), true)\\n+\\tif found.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tvar plain := _find_entry_block(lines, entry_id(server_name), false)\\n+\\t\\tif not plain.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\t## A plain `- id: mcp-godot-ai` row is inert for a new id — the\\n+\\t\\t\\t## loader skips it with a warning and never registers the server.\\n+\\t\\t\\t## Replacing the whole row with the insert form migrates it.\\n+\\t\\t\\tfound = plain\\n+\\tif not found.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t## Pass the parsed config through so user-mutable fields (env,\\n+\\t\\t## toolCallTimeoutMs, reconnect) survive a reconfigure.\\n+\\t\\texisting_config = _extract_config(lines, found)\\n+\\tvar new_entry := build_entry(client, server_name, server_url, existing_config, launch)\\n+\\t## Nested replacement must keep the sequence's existing header indent —\\n+\\t## items of one block sequence share indentation, so a hand-written\\n+\\t## 2-space file re-emitted at 4 spaces would no longer parse (#867\\n+\\t## review). Fresh rows and plain-row migrations keep the 4-space style.\\n+\\tvar base_indent := ENTRY_INDENT\\n+\\tif not found.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tvar header_indent := int(found.get(\\\"indent\\\", 0))\\n+\\t\\tif header_indent > 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\tbase_indent = header_indent\\n+\\tvar rendered := render_nested_entry(entry_id(server_name), new_entry, base_indent)\\n+\\tvar out := \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tif found.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tout = _append_row(lines, rendered)\\n+\\telse:\\n+\\t\\tout = _replace_lines(lines, found, rendered)\\n+\\tif not McpAtomicWrite.write(path, out):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Cannot write to %s\\\" % path}\\n+\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\", \\\"message\\\": McpClient.configured_message(client, server_url)}\\n+\\n+\\n+static func check_status(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary = {},\\n+) -> McpClient.Status:\\n+\\treturn check_status_details(client, server_name, server_url, launch)[\\\"status\\\"]\\n+\\n+\\n+## Same contract as the other strategies (#711): {status, error_msg}. An\\n+## existing-but-unreadable config is ERROR with the diagnostic — not\\n+## NOT_CONFIGURED — so the dock row can tell \\\"no config\\\" from \\\"config the\\n+## editor can't read\\\" instead of offering a Configure that would fail.\\n+static func check_status_details(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient, server_name: String, server_url: String, launch: Dictionary = {}\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar resolution := client.resolved_config_path_details()\\n+\\tvar path := str(resolution.get(\\\"path\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tvar path_error := str(resolution.get(\\\"error\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tif not path_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.ERROR, \\\"error_msg\\\": path_error}\\n+\\tif path.is_empty() or not FileAccess.file_exists(path):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED, \\\"error_msg\\\": \\\"\\\"}\\n+\\tvar read := _read(path)\\n+\\tif not read[\\\"ok\\\"]:\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.ERROR,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"error_msg\\\": \\\"Cannot read %s: %s\\\" % [path, read[\\\"error\\\"]],\\n+\\t\\t}\\n+\\tvar text := String(read[\\\"data\\\"])\\n+\\tif not _is_patch_sequence(text):\\n+\\t\\t## A top-level mapping/scalar is not a dsh patch list: nothing in it\\n+\\t\\t## can be \\\"configured\\\", and a nested `- id:` inside a mapping must\\n+\\t\\t## not read as ours (#867 review).\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.ERROR,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"error_msg\\\": \\\"%s is not a top-level YAML list of loader entries; fix or remove the file.\\\" % path,\\n+\\t\\t}\\n+\\tvar lines := _split_lines(text)\\n+\\tvar found := _find_entry_block(lines, entry_id(server_name), true)\\n+\\tif found.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t## A plain row for our id is inert (the loader skips unknown ids), so\\n+\\t\\t## the server is NOT registered no matter what the file says. It only\\n+\\t\\t## becomes registered once Configure rewrites it as an insert row.\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED, \\\"error_msg\\\": \\\"\\\"}\\n+\\tvar config := _extract_config(lines, found)\\n+\\tif config.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED, \\\"error_msg\\\": \\\"\\\"}\\n+\\t## An entry exists but no verified launcher does — mirror the other\\n+\\t## strategies: this is an environment ERROR, not entry drift.\\n+\\tvar launch_error := command_launch_error(client, launch)\\n+\\tif not launch_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.ERROR, \\\"error_msg\\\": launch_error}\\n+\\tif verify_entry(client, config, server_name, server_url, launch):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.CONFIGURED, \\\"error_msg\\\": \\\"\\\"}\\n+\\treturn {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.CONFIGURED_MISMATCH,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"error_msg\\\": \\\"\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\\"owned\\\": McpClient.launch_values_mention_godot_ai(McpClient.entry_launch_values(config)),\\n+\\t}\\n+\\n+\\n+static func remove(client: McpClient, server_name: String) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar resolution := client.resolved_config_path_details()\\n+\\tvar path := str(resolution.get(\\\"path\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tvar path_error := str(resolution.get(\\\"error\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tif path_error.is_empty() and not path.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tpath_error = McpClient.unshared_flatpak_config_error(client.display_name, path)\\n+\\tif not path_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": path_error}\\n+\\tif path.is_empty() or not FileAccess.file_exists(path):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Not configured\\\"}\\n+\\tvar read := _read(path)\\n+\\tif not read[\\\"ok\\\"]:\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Refusing to rewrite %s: %s.\\\" % [path, read[\\\"error\\\"]]}\\n+\\tvar text := String(read[\\\"data\\\"])\\n+\\tif not _is_patch_sequence(text):\\n+\\t\\t## Same fail-closed contract as configure (#867 review): never modify\\n+\\t\\t## a file that dsh cannot load as a patch list.\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"message\\\": \\\"Refusing to rewrite %s: it is not a top-level YAML list of loader entries. Fix or remove the file, then re-run Remove.\\\" % path,\\n+\\t\\t}\\n+\\tvar lines := _split_lines(text)\\n+\\tvar found := _find_entry_block(lines, entry_id(server_name), true)\\n+\\tif found.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tfound = _find_entry_block(lines, entry_id(server_name), false)\\n+\\tif found.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"%s configuration removed\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\tvar out := _remove_lines(lines, found)\\n+\\tif _is_blank_or_comment_only(out):\\n+\\t\\t## dsh's patch parser requires a top-level YAML array: an empty or\\n+\\t\\t## comment-only patch file FAILS BOOT (`loadOptionalPatches` throws on\\n+\\t\\t## a non-array file), while an absent file means \\\"no layer\\\". After our\\n+\\t\\t## row is gone, an all-blank file must therefore be deleted, not\\n+\\t\\t## written back.\\n+\\t\\tif FileAccess.file_exists(path):\\n+\\t\\t\\tvar remove_err := DirAccess.remove_absolute(path)\\n+\\t\\t\\tif remove_err != OK:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Cannot remove %s (error %d)\\\" % [path, remove_err]}\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"%s configuration removed\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\tif not McpAtomicWrite.write(path, out):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Cannot write to %s\\\" % path}\\n+\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"%s configuration removed\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\n+\\n+## Build the `config` dict written inside the loader entry. Command mode\\n+## (FLAT, #838) nests `serverName`/`transport`/`command`/`args`; URL mode nests\\n+## `serverName`/`transport`/`url`. Existing user state (env, timeouts,\\n+## reconnect) survives a reconfigure via the deep copy.\\n+static func build_entry(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\texisting: Variant = null,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary = {},\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar entry: Dictionary = (existing as Dictionary).duplicate(true) if existing is Dictionary else {}\\n+\\tentry[\\\"serverName\\\"] = server_name\\n+\\tif client.command_shape == McpClient.CommandShape.FLAT:\\n+\\t\\tentry[\\\"command\\\"] = str(launch.get(\\\"command\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\t\\tentry[\\\"args\\\"] = _array_copy(launch.get(\\\"args\\\", []))\\n+\\t\\tif not client.command_transport_key.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\tentry[client.command_transport_key] = client.command_transport_value\\n+\\t\\tfor key in client.command_initial_fields:\\n+\\t\\t\\tif not entry.has(key):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tentry[key] = client.command_initial_fields[key]\\n+\\t\\tfor key in client.command_legacy_keys:\\n+\\t\\t\\tentry.erase(String(key))\\n+\\t\\treturn entry\\n+\\tif client.command_shape == McpClient.CommandShape.NONE:\\n+\\t\\t## Single URL-mode source of truth (#867 review): build_entry's URL\\n+\\t\\t## branch delegates to build_url_entry so the two can never drift.\\n+\\t\\treturn build_url_entry(client, server_name, server_url, existing)\\n+\\treturn {}\\n+\\n+\\n+## URL-mode sibling for the manual-instruction fallback text and the\\n+## build_entry URL branch. DeepSeek Harness' mcp-client bridge accepts\\n+## `transport: streamable-http` with a `url`, so the entry keeps the same\\n+## `serverName` namespace with an HTTP transport instead of the stdio attach\\n+## bridge. Existing user state survives via the deep copy.\\n+static func build_url_entry(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient, server_name: String, server_url: String, existing: Variant = null\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar entry: Dictionary = (existing as Dictionary).duplicate(true) if existing is Dictionary else {}\\n+\\tentry[\\\"serverName\\\"] = server_name\\n+\\tentry[\\\"transport\\\"] = \\\"streamable-http\\\"\\n+\\tif not client.entry_url_field.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tentry[client.entry_url_field] = server_url\\n+\\treturn entry\\n+\\n+\\n+## Verify a stored `config` dict matches the current launch. Command mode\\n+## checks every launch-affecting value exactly and requires legacy URL keys\\n+## gone; serverName must match the pinned namespace. URL mode checks the url\\n+## and the streamable-http pin.\\n+static func verify_entry(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tconfig: Dictionary,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary = {},\\n+) -> bool:\\n+\\tif config.get(\\\"serverName\\\") != server_name:\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tif client.command_shape != McpClient.CommandShape.NONE:\\n+\\t\\tif client.command_shape != McpClient.CommandShape.FLAT or not bool(launch.get(\\\"ok\\\", false)):\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\t\\tfor key in client.command_legacy_keys:\\n+\\t\\t\\tif config.has(String(key)):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\t\\tif config.get(\\\"command\\\") != launch.get(\\\"command\\\"):\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\t\\tif not _arrays_equal(config.get(\\\"args\\\", null), launch.get(\\\"args\\\", null)):\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\t\\tif not client.command_transport_key.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\tif config.get(client.command_transport_key, null) != client.command_transport_value:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\t\\treturn true\\n+\\tif config.get(client.entry_url_field, \\\"\\\") != server_url:\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\treturn config.get(\\\"transport\\\", \\\"\\\") == \\\"streamable-http\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+## Empty string when this client's launch requirements are satisfied.\\n+## Mirrors `McpYamlStrategy.command_launch_error`; the patch-list dialect\\n+## supports FLAT (and the URL fallback) only.\\n+static func command_launch_error(client: McpClient, launch: Dictionary) -> String:\\n+\\tif client.command_shape == McpClient.CommandShape.NONE:\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tif client.command_shape != McpClient.CommandShape.FLAT:\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"%s uses a command shape not supported by DeepSeek Harness config yet\\\" % client.display_name\\n+\\tif not bool(launch.get(\\\"ok\\\", false)):\\n+\\t\\treturn str(launch.get(\\\"error\\\", \\\"No compatible attach launcher was found.\\\"))\\n+\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+# --- Rendering --------------------------------------------------------------\\n+\\n+## Render a full `- insert:` row containing one nested loader entry. Public\\n+## seam for the dock's manual-instruction text so the pasted YAML matches\\n+## what Configure would write byte-for-byte.\\n+static func render_insert_row(entry_id_value: String, config: Dictionary) -> PackedStringArray:\\n+\\tvar lines: PackedStringArray = [\\\"- insert:\\\"]\\n+\\tlines.append_array(render_nested_entry(entry_id_value, config))\\n+\\treturn lines\\n+\\n+\\n+## Render one nested loader entry (the `- id:` block inside an `insert`\\n+## list). `base_indent` defaults to the 4-space style used by the shipped\\n+## patch files; a nested replacement reuses the indent the existing entry was\\n+## found at so the block sequence keeps a single indentation (#867 review).\\n+static func render_nested_entry(\\n+\\tentry_id_value: String, config: Dictionary, base_indent: int = ENTRY_INDENT\\n+) -> PackedStringArray:\\n+\\tvar lines: PackedStringArray = []\\n+\\tlines.append(\\\"%s- id: %s\\\" % [_indent_str(base_indent), entry_id_value])\\n+\\tlines.append(\\\"%sname: '%s'\\\" % [_indent_str(base_indent + 2), PLUGIN_NAME])\\n+\\tlines.append(\\\"%sconfig:\\\" % _indent_str(base_indent + 2))\\n+\\tfor key in config:\\n+\\t\\tlines.append_array(_emit_field(String(key), config[key], base_indent + 4))\\n+\\treturn lines\\n+\\n+\\n+## Emit one `key: value` line (or a nested block for Dictionary values) at\\n+## the given indent. Arrays render in flow style; scalars through the shared\\n+## YAML emitter so quoting rules stay identical across the two YAML dialects.\\n+static func _emit_field(key: String, value: Variant, indent: int) -> PackedStringArray:\\n+\\tvar lines: PackedStringArray = []\\n+\\tvar prefix := _indent_str(indent)\\n+\\tif value is Dictionary:\\n+\\t\\tlines.append(\\\"%s%s:\\\" % [prefix, key])\\n+\\t\\tfor sub_key in value:\\n+\\t\\t\\tlines.append_array(_emit_field(String(sub_key), value[sub_key], indent + 2))\\n+\\telif value is Array or value is PackedStringArray:\\n+\\t\\tlines.append(\\\"%s%s: %s\\\" % [prefix, key, McpYamlStrategy.emit_flow_array(_array_copy(value))])\\n+\\telse:\\n+\\t\\tlines.append(\\\"%s%s: %s\\\" % [prefix, key, McpYamlStrategy.emit_scalar(value)])\\n+\\treturn lines\\n+\\n+\\n+# --- Line-level parsing (no YAML parser in Godot) ---------------------------\\n+\\n+## Split file text into lines, dropping only a single trailing newline so the\\n+## remainder round-trips byte-for-byte. `text` is never split on the empty\\n+## string, so an empty file yields an empty array.\\n+static func _split_lines(text: String) -> PackedStringArray:\\n+\\tif text.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn PackedStringArray()\\n+\\tvar lines := text.split(\\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n+\\tif lines.size() > 0 and String(lines[lines.size() - 1]).is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tlines.remove_at(lines.size() - 1)\\n+\\treturn lines\\n+\\n+\\n+## Locate the block owned by our loader entry:\\n+##   require_nested=true  — the `- id: <entry_id>` line inside an `insert`\\n+##                         list (indent > 0), plus its indented children.\\n+##   require_nested=false — a plain top-level `- id: <entry_id>` row (indent\\n+##                         0), plus its indented children.\\n+## Returns {\\\"start\\\": int, \\\"end\\\": int, \\\"indent\\\": int} (end exclusive) or {}\\n+## when absent. The header indent is detected dynamically so a hand-written\\n+## file using non-2-space indentation is still found; children are lines\\n+## deeper than the header. `end` stops right AFTER the last content line:\\n+## trailing blank and comment lines between our entry and the next sibling\\n+## belong to the file, not to our block, so `_replace_lines` / `_remove_lines`\\n+## (which splice [start, end)) never delete them (#867 review).\\n+static func _find_entry_block(lines: PackedStringArray, entry_id_value: String, require_nested: bool) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tfor i in range(lines.size()):\\n+\\t\\tvar indent := _indent_of(lines[i])\\n+\\t\\tif require_nested and indent <= 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tif not require_nested and indent != 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tvar parsed := _parse_entry_header(lines[i])\\n+\\t\\tif parsed.is_empty() or parsed.get(\\\"id\\\") != entry_id_value:\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\t## A nested match must actually live inside an `- insert:` row: a\\n+\\t\\t## `- id:` list under some other top-level row (an override row's own\\n+\\t\\t## sequence, say) is not a loader entry and is not ours to touch.\\n+\\t\\tif require_nested and not _under_insert_row(lines, i):\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tvar j := i + 1\\n+\\t\\tvar last_content := i\\n+\\t\\twhile j < lines.size():\\n+\\t\\t\\tvar l := lines[j]\\n+\\t\\t\\tif _is_blank_or_comment(l):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tj += 1\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\t\\tif _indent_of(l) <= indent:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n+\\t\\t\\tlast_content = j\\n+\\t\\t\\tj += 1\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"start\\\": i, \\\"end\\\": last_content + 1, \\\"indent\\\": indent}\\n+\\treturn {}\\n+\\n+\\n+## True when the nearest preceding 0-indent content line above `index` is an\\n+## `- insert:` row header. Blank and comment lines are skipped; hitting the\\n+## top of the file without a header means the nested line is orphaned and\\n+## cannot be a loader entry.\\n+static func _under_insert_row(lines: PackedStringArray, index: int) -> bool:\\n+\\tfor i in range(index - 1, -1, -1):\\n+\\t\\tif _is_blank_or_comment(lines[i]):\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tif _indent_of(lines[i]) == 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn lines[i].strip_edges().begins_with(\\\"- insert:\\\")\\n+\\treturn false\\n+\\n+\\n+## Parse a `- id: <value>` header line into {\\\"id\\\": String}. Returns {} for\\n+## any other line. The value may be plain or single/double-quoted.\\n+static func _parse_entry_header(line: String) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar stripped := line.strip_edges()\\n+\\tif not stripped.begins_with(\\\"- \\\"):\\n+\\t\\treturn {}\\n+\\tvar body := stripped.substr(2).strip_edges()\\n+\\tif not body.begins_with(\\\"id:\\\"):\\n+\\t\\treturn {}\\n+\\tvar value := body.substr(3).strip_edges()\\n+\\tif value.begins_with(\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\") and value.ends_with(\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\") and value.length() >= 2:\\n+\\t\\tvar parsed: Variant = JSON.parse_string(value)\\n+\\t\\tif parsed is String:\\n+\\t\\t\\tvalue = parsed\\n+\\telif value.begins_with(\\\"'\\\") and value.ends_with(\\\"'\\\") and value.length() >= 2:\\n+\\t\\tvalue = value.substr(1, value.length() - 2)\\n+\\treturn {\\\"id\\\": value}\\n+\\n+\\n+## Extract the `config:` sub-dict from a found entry block. Fields may nest\\n+## (env, reconnect); deeper values are parsed with the same scalar coercion\\n+## the YAML strategy uses. Returns {} when the block has no config block.\\n+static func _extract_config(lines: PackedStringArray, block: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar start := int(block.get(\\\"start\\\", -1))\\n+\\tvar end := int(block.get(\\\"end\\\", -1))\\n+\\tif start < 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn {}\\n+\\tvar config_indent := -1\\n+\\tvar config_start := -1\\n+\\tfor i in range(start, end):\\n+\\t\\tvar stripped := lines[i].strip_edges()\\n+\\t\\tif _is_blank_or_comment(lines[i]):\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tif stripped == \\\"config:\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\tconfig_indent = _indent_of(lines[i])\\n+\\t\\t\\tconfig_start = i\\n+\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n+\\tif config_start < 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn {}\\n+\\treturn _parse_block_lines(lines, config_start + 1, end, config_indent)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Parse sibling `key: value` lines at a fixed minimum indent into a\\n+## Dictionary. Nested blocks (a key with no inline value) recurse and their\\n+## child lines are consumed by the recursion — the parent index advances past\\n+## them so they are never re-parsed as top-level config fields (#867 review).\\n+## Flow arrays parse back into Arrays via JSON. Quoted and plain scalars\\n+## strip via the YAML strategy's shared `coerce_scalar`.\\n+static func _parse_block_lines(lines: PackedStringArray, start: int, end: int, min_indent: int) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar out: Dictionary = {}\\n+\\tvar i := start\\n+\\twhile i < end:\\n+\\t\\tvar l := lines[i]\\n+\\t\\tif _is_blank_or_comment(l):\\n+\\t\\t\\ti += 1\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tvar indent := _indent_of(l)\\n+\\t\\tif indent <= min_indent:\\n+\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n+\\t\\tvar stripped := l.strip_edges()\\n+\\t\\tvar colon := stripped.find(\\\":\\\")\\n+\\t\\tif colon < 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\ti += 1\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tvar key := stripped.substr(0, colon).strip_edges()\\n+\\t\\tvar value := stripped.substr(colon + 1).strip_edges()\\n+\\t\\tif value.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\t## Nested block: find where its children end (a line at or above\\n+\\t\\t\\t## the block key's indent), parse only that span, and jump past it.\\n+\\t\\t\\tvar child_end := i + 1\\n+\\t\\t\\twhile child_end < end:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tif not _is_blank_or_comment(lines[child_end]) and _indent_of(lines[child_end]) <= indent:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tchild_end += 1\\n+\\t\\t\\tout[key] = _parse_block_lines(lines, i + 1, child_end, indent)\\n+\\t\\t\\ti = child_end\\n+\\t\\telse:\\n+\\t\\t\\t## Shared with the YAML strategy so the two dialects can never\\n+\\t\\t\\t## drift on quoting/typing rules (#867 review).\\n+\\t\\t\\tout[key] = McpYamlStrategy.coerce_scalar(value)\\n+\\t\\t\\ti += 1\\n+\\treturn out\\n+\\n+\\n+# --- Rewrites ---------------------------------------------------------------\\n+\\n+## Append a new `- insert:` row at the end of the file. A blank separator\\n+## line keeps rows visually distinct; the file always ends with a newline.\\n+static func _append_row(lines: PackedStringArray, nested_lines: PackedStringArray) -> String:\\n+\\tvar parts: Array[String] = []\\n+\\tfor l in lines:\\n+\\t\\tparts.append(l)\\n+\\t## Collapse trailing blank lines so the appended row is not orphaned\\n+\\t## below stray empties; one separator blank keeps rows readable.\\n+\\twhile parts.size() > 0 and String(parts[parts.size() - 1]).strip_edges().is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tparts.remove_at(parts.size() - 1)\\n+\\tif parts.size() > 0:\\n+\\t\\tparts.append(\\\"\\\")\\n+\\tparts.append(\\\"- insert:\\\")\\n+\\tfor l in nested_lines:\\n+\\t\\tparts.append(l)\\n+\\treturn _join_lines(parts)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Replace the lines owned by our entry. When the found block is a plain\\n+## top-level row (indent 0 header), the whole row — including its children —\\n+## is replaced by the insert form. When it is a nested block inside an\\n+## insert row, only that block's lines are replaced, leaving sibling entries\\n+## and comments in the same row byte-for-byte intact.\\n+static func _replace_lines(lines: PackedStringArray, block: Dictionary, nested_lines: PackedStringArray) -> String:\\n+\\tvar start := int(block.get(\\\"start\\\", -1))\\n+\\tvar end := int(block.get(\\\"end\\\", -1))\\n+\\tvar parts: Array[String] = []\\n+\\tfor i in range(lines.size()):\\n+\\t\\tif i == start:\\n+\\t\\t\\t## A nested block keeps the surrounding `- insert:` row, so only\\n+\\t\\t\\t## the nested entry lines (indent > 0) are spliced in.\\n+\\t\\t\\tif _indent_of(lines[start]) > 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tfor l in nested_lines:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\tparts.append(l)\\n+\\t\\t\\telse:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tparts.append(\\\"- insert:\\\")\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tfor l in nested_lines:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\tparts.append(l)\\n+\\t\\tif i >= start and i < end:\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tparts.append(lines[i])\\n+\\treturn _join_lines(parts)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Remove our entry. A nested block is spliced out; if its enclosing insert\\n+## row is left without any nested entries (only the bare `- insert:` header,\\n+## blanks, and comments), the row header goes too so the file stays a valid\\n+## patch list. A plain row for our id is removed wholesale.\\n+static func _remove_lines(lines: PackedStringArray, block: Dictionary) -> String:\\n+\\tvar start := int(block.get(\\\"start\\\", -1))\\n+\\tvar end := int(block.get(\\\"end\\\", -1))\\n+\\tvar nested := _indent_of(lines[start]) > 0\\n+\\tvar parts: Array[String] = []\\n+\\tif nested:\\n+\\t\\t## Find the enclosing `- insert:` row header: the nearest preceding\\n+\\t\\t## 0-indent `- ` line.\\n+\\t\\tvar row_start := -1\\n+\\t\\tfor i in range(start - 1, -1, -1):\\n+\\t\\t\\tif _indent_of(lines[i]) == 0 and lines[i].strip_edges().begins_with(\\\"- \\\"):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\trow_start = i\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n+\\t\\t## Row end: first 0-indent non-blank/comment line after the block.\\n+\\t\\tvar row_end := lines.size()\\n+\\t\\tfor i in range(end, lines.size()):\\n+\\t\\t\\tif _indent_of(lines[i]) == 0 and not _is_blank_or_comment(lines[i]):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\trow_end = i\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n+\\t\\tvar has_other_entries := false\\n+\\t\\tfor i in range(row_start + 1, row_end):\\n+\\t\\t\\tif i >= start and i < end:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\t\\tif _is_blank_or_comment(lines[i]):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\t\\tif _indent_of(lines[i]) > 0 and lines[i].strip_edges().begins_with(\\\"- \\\"):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\thas_other_entries = true\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n+\\t\\tif has_other_entries or row_start < 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\t## Only our block goes; sibling entries and any trailing\\n+\\t\\t\\t## comments in the same row survive.\\n+\\t\\t\\tfor i in range(lines.size()):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tif i >= start and i < end:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tparts.append(lines[i])\\n+\\t\\telse:\\n+\\t\\t\\t## The row held only our entry: drop the `- insert:` header and\\n+\\t\\t\\t## our block, but keep trailing blank/comment lines — a comment\\n+\\t\\t\\t## the user wrote above the next row must survive (#867 review).\\n+\\t\\t\\tfor i in range(lines.size()):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tif i >= row_start and i < end:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tparts.append(lines[i])\\n+\\t\\t\\tparts = _trim_trailing_blanks(parts)\\n+\\telse:\\n+\\t\\t## Plain top-level row for our id: drop the whole block.\\n+\\t\\tfor i in range(lines.size()):\\n+\\t\\t\\tif i >= start and i < end:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\t\\tparts.append(lines[i])\\n+\\t\\tparts = _trim_trailing_blanks(parts)\\n+\\treturn _join_lines(parts)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Remove trailing blank lines left behind by a removal.\\n+static func _trim_trailing_blanks(parts: Array[String]) -> Array[String]:\\n+\\twhile parts.size() > 0 and String(parts[parts.size() - 1]).strip_edges().is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tparts.remove_at(parts.size() - 1)\\n+\\treturn parts\\n+\\n+\\n+## True when the text holds no patch rows at all — blank lines and comments\\n+## only. Such a file cannot be written back (see `remove`), because dsh's\\n+## patch parser rejects a non-array file at boot.\\n+static func _is_blank_or_comment_only(text: String) -> bool:\\n+\\tfor line in text.split(\\\"\\\\n\\\"):\\n+\\t\\tvar stripped := line.strip_edges()\\n+\\t\\tif not stripped.is_empty() and not stripped.begins_with(\\\"#\\\"):\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\treturn true\\n+\\n+\\n+## When the document's only content line is the empty flow sequence `[]`,\\n+## return the text with that one line removed — blanks and comments stay,\\n+## because they belong to the user. Any other document comes back unchanged;\\n+## a `[]` mixed with real rows is left for `_is_patch_sequence` to refuse.\\n+static func _strip_empty_flow_sequence(text: String) -> String:\\n+\\tvar lines := _split_lines(text)\\n+\\tvar bracket_line := -1\\n+\\tfor i in range(lines.size()):\\n+\\t\\tif _is_blank_or_comment(lines[i]):\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tif bracket_line < 0 and lines[i].strip_edges() == \\\"[]\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\tbracket_line = i\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\treturn text\\n+\\tif bracket_line < 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn text\\n+\\tvar parts: Array[String] = []\\n+\\tfor i in range(lines.size()):\\n+\\t\\tif i != bracket_line:\\n+\\t\\t\\tparts.append(lines[i])\\n+\\tif parts.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\treturn _join_lines(parts)\\n+\\n+\\n+## True when the text is a valid loader patch list (or empty/comment-only /\\n+## the empty flow sequence `[]`, which all mean \\\"no layer\\\"). Anything else —\\n+## a top-level mapping or scalar — would make dsh fail boot, so Configure\\n+## refuses to write into it instead of producing a broken file (#867 review).\\n+## Only 0-indent content lines define the top-level structure: they must all\\n+## be `- ` rows. Deeper lines are row children and are not inspected. `[]` is\\n+## valid only as the COMPLETE document — appending rows after the empty flow\\n+## sequence would produce malformed YAML, so a mixed `[]` + rows file fails\\n+## the check too.\\n+static func _is_patch_sequence(text: String) -> bool:\\n+\\tif text.strip_edges().is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn true\\n+\\tvar saw_brackets := false\\n+\\tvar content_count := 0\\n+\\tfor line in text.split(\\\"\\\\n\\\"):\\n+\\t\\tvar stripped := line.strip_edges()\\n+\\t\\tif stripped.is_empty() or stripped.begins_with(\\\"#\\\"):\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tcontent_count += 1\\n+\\t\\tif stripped == \\\"[]\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\tsaw_brackets = true\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tif _indent_of(line) == 0 and not stripped.begins_with(\\\"- \\\"):\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tif saw_brackets and content_count > 1:\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\treturn true\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _join_lines(parts: Array) -> String:\\n+\\tif parts.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tvar lines: Array[String] = []\\n+\\tfor p in parts:\\n+\\t\\tlines.append(String(p))\\n+\\treturn \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(lines) + \\\"\\\\n\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+# --- Small helpers ----------------------------------------------------------\\n+\\n+static func _indent_str(n: int) -> String:\\n+\\tvar out := \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tfor i in range(n):\\n+\\t\\tout += \\\" \\\"\\n+\\treturn out\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _indent_of(line: String) -> int:\\n+\\tvar n := 0\\n+\\twhile n < line.length() and (line[n] == \\\" \\\" or line[n] == \\\"\\\\t\\\"):\\n+\\t\\tn += 1\\n+\\treturn n\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _is_blank_or_comment(line: String) -> bool:\\n+\\tvar stripped := line.strip_edges()\\n+\\treturn stripped.is_empty() or stripped.begins_with(\\\"#\\\")\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _array_copy(value: Variant) -> Array:\\n+\\tif value is Array:\\n+\\t\\treturn (value as Array).duplicate(true)\\n+\\tif value is PackedStringArray:\\n+\\t\\treturn McpClient._array_from_packed(value)\\n+\\treturn []\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _arrays_equal(left: Variant, right: Variant) -> bool:\\n+\\tif not (left is Array or left is PackedStringArray):\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tif not (right is Array or right is PackedStringArray):\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tvar left_array := _array_copy(left)\\n+\\tvar right_array := _array_copy(right)\\n+\\tif left_array.size() != right_array.size():\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tfor i in range(left_array.size()):\\n+\\t\\tif left_array[i] != right_array[i]:\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\treturn true\\n+\\n+\\n+## Returns {\\\"ok\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": String} when the file is absent or readable,\\n+## and {\\\"ok\\\": false, \\\"error\\\": String} when unreadable. Callers must NOT fall\\n+## back to an empty string on the error path — doing so blows away the user's\\n+## other patch rows on the next write.\\n+static func _read(path: String) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tif not FileAccess.file_exists(path):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"ok\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": \\\"\\\"}\\n+\\tvar f := FileAccess.open(path, FileAccess.READ)\\n+\\tif f == null:\\n+\\t\\tvar err := FileAccess.get_open_error()\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"ok\\\": false, \\\"error\\\": \\\"could not open for reading (%s)\\\" % error_string(err)}\\n+\\tvar t := f.get_as_text()\\n+\\tf.close()\\n+\\treturn {\\\"ok\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": t}\"},{\"sha\":\"36703f43cb1cf567db5c85af3ba8f8dff743655b\",\"filename\":\"addons/godot_ai/clients/_dsh_strategy.gd.uid\",\"status\":\"added\",\"additions\":1,\"deletions\":0,\"changes\":1,\"blob_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/blob/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_dsh_strategy.gd.uid\",\"raw_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/raw/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_dsh_strategy.gd.uid\",\"contents_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/contents/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_dsh_strategy.gd.uid?ref=3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318\",\"patch\":\"@@ -0,0 +1,1252 @@\\n+@tool\\n+class_name McpJsonStrategy\\n+extends RefCounted\\n+\\n+## Read–merge–write strategy for JSON-backed MCP clients.\\n+## All knobs come from the McpClient descriptor as plain data — no Callables.\\n+## See `_base.gd` for why descriptors are data-only.\\n+\\n+\\n+static func configure(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary = {},\\n+\\tproject_roots: PackedStringArray = PackedStringArray(),\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tif _uses_merge_tiers(client):\\n+\\t\\treturn _configure_merged(client, server_name, server_url, launch, project_roots)\\n+\\tvar resolution := client.resolved_config_path_details()\\n+\\tvar path := str(resolution.get(\\\"path\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tvar path_error := str(resolution.get(\\\"error\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tif path_error.is_empty() and not path.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tpath_error = McpClient.unshared_flatpak_config_error(client.display_name, path)\\n+\\tif not path_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": path_error}\\n+\\tif path.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Could not resolve config path for %s on this OS\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\t## Set only while no candidate file exists, so this never blocks an update.\\n+\\tvar create_error := str(resolution.get(\\\"create_error\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tif not create_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": create_error}\\n+\\n+\\tvar seed_path := str(resolution.get(\\\"seed_path\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tvar read_path := seed_path if not FileAccess.file_exists(path) and not seed_path.is_empty() else path\\n+\\tvar read := _read_or_init(read_path)\\n+\\tif not read[\\\"ok\\\"]:\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Refusing to overwrite %s: %s. Fix or move the file, then re-run Configure.\\\" % [read_path, read[\\\"error\\\"]]}\\n+\\tvar launch_error := command_launch_error(client, launch)\\n+\\tif not launch_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": launch_error}\\n+\\tvar config: Dictionary = read[\\\"data\\\"]\\n+\\tvar holder := _ensure_path(config, select_server_key_path(config, client))\\n+\\t## Pass the existing entry through so `build_entry` can preserve user-mutable\\n+\\t## state (auto-approval lists, `disabled` toggles) instead of resetting it\\n+\\t## to descriptor defaults on every Configure click. See `entry_initial_fields`\\n+\\t## docs in `_base.gd`.\\n+\\tvar existing: Variant = holder.get(server_name, null)\\n+\\tholder[server_name] = build_entry(client, server_url, existing, launch)\\n+\\n+\\t# F5: refuse to re-serialize a file whose parsed integers above 2^53 would\\n+\\t# lose precision. Editing by hand keeps those values intact.\\n+\\tif _has_lossy_numbers(config):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Refusing to rewrite %s: contains integers above 2^53 that Godot's JSON parser would re-emit imprecise. Edit the file by hand.\\\" % path}\\n+\\tif not McpAtomicWrite.write(path, JSON.stringify(_narrow_integral_numbers(config), \\\"\\\\t\\\", false)):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Cannot write to %s\\\" % path}\\n+\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\", \\\"message\\\": McpClient.configured_message(client, server_url)}\\n+\\n+## Pi-style clients merge several global config files. Update the effective\\n+## highest-precedence definition; fail closed when a project override exists\\n+## because Pi's external working directory cannot be inferred safely here.\\n+static func _configure_merged(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary,\\n+\\tproject_roots: PackedStringArray,\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar write_error := _merge_write_error(client)\\n+\\tif not write_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": write_error}\\n+\\tvar launch_error := command_launch_error(client, launch)\\n+\\tif not launch_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": launch_error}\\n+\\t## A matched `config_scope_globs` directory (omp named profiles) means the\\n+\\t## user scope may live elsewhere for the running client; writing the\\n+\\t## default tiers would false-succeed (#1085).\\n+\\tvar scope_error := _scope_ambiguity_error(client)\\n+\\tif not scope_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": scope_error}\\n+\\tvar project := _load_project_definitions(client, server_name, project_roots)\\n+\\tif not project.get(\\\"ok\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": str(project.get(\\\"error\\\", \\\"Cannot inspect project config tiers\\\"))}\\n+\\tvar project_tiers: Array = project.get(\\\"tiers\\\", [])\\n+\\tif not project_tiers.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": _project_override_message(project_tiers, \\\"update or remove\\\", client.display_name, server_name)}\\n+\\tvar loaded := _load_merge_tiers(client)\\n+\\tif not loaded.get(\\\"ok\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": str(loaded.get(\\\"error\\\", \\\"Cannot read merged config tiers\\\"))}\\n+\\tvar tiers: Array = loaded.get(\\\"tiers\\\", [])\\n+\\tif tiers.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Could not resolve config path for %s on this OS\\\" % client.display_name}\\n+\\tvar denylist_error := _denylist_error(client, tiers, server_name)\\n+\\tif not denylist_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": denylist_error}\\n+\\tvar target_index := 0\\n+\\tfor index in range(tiers.size()):\\n+\\t\\tvar config: Dictionary = tiers[index][\\\"data\\\"]\\n+\\t\\tvar holder := _walk_path(config, select_server_key_path(config, client))\\n+\\t\\tif holder is Dictionary and holder.has(server_name):\\n+\\t\\t\\ttarget_index = index\\n+\\t\\t\\t## First-definition-wins clients keep the earliest defining tier;\\n+\\t\\t\\t## later tiers are dead, so updating one of those changes nothing.\\n+\\t\\t\\tif _first_wins(client):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n+\\tvar tier: Dictionary = tiers[target_index]\\n+\\tvar config: Dictionary = tier[\\\"data\\\"]\\n+\\tvar holder := _ensure_path(config, select_server_key_path(config, client))\\n+\\tvar existing: Variant = holder.get(server_name, null)\\n+\\tvar disabled_error := _disabled_entry_error(client, tiers, server_name, existing, str(tier[\\\"path\\\"]))\\n+\\tif not disabled_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": disabled_error}\\n+\\tholder[server_name] = build_entry(client, server_url, existing, launch)\\n+\\tvar path := str(tier[\\\"path\\\"])\\n+\\t# F5: refuse to re-serialize a tier whose parsed integers above 2^53 would\\n+\\t# lose precision. Edit the file by hand.\\\" % path}\\n+\\tif not McpAtomicWrite.write(path, JSON.stringify(_narrow_integral_numbers(config), \\\"\\\\t\\\", false)):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"error\\\", \\\"message\\\": \\\"Cannot write to %s\\\" % path}\\n+\\t# Codex F1 caveat: when we wrote a global tier but couldn't find any project\\n+\\t# override in the roots we probed AND the user hasn't told us where Pi (or any\\n+\\t# external cwd client) actually runs from, we can't prove this write is the\\n+\\t# effective config. (we fail closed there), so\\n+\\t# reaching this point with `project_tiers.is_empty()` means: no probed\\n+\\t# override was found.\\n+\\tvar message := McpClient.configured_message(client, server_url)\\n+\\tvar external_cwd := str(McpClientConfigurator._editor_setting_lookup(McpClientConfigurator.SETTING_EXTERNAL_CLIENT_CWD))\\n+\\tif external_cwd.is_empty() and project_tiers.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tmessage += \\\" If %s is launched from a cwd this editor cannot see, the effective entry may still be in a project-tier file — set godot_ai/external_client_cwd to that cwd to verify.\\\" % client.display_name\\n+\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"ok\\\", \\\"message\\\": message}\\n+\\n+\\n+static func check_status(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: server_name, server_url, launch, project_roots).get(\\\"status\\\", McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Detailed variant feeding the dock's error_msg plumbing (#711): a config\\n+## file that EXISTS but can't be read or parsed is Status.ERROR carrying the\\n+## read/parse error, not NOT_CONFIGURED — the write path refuses to touch\\n+## such a file (see `_read_or_init`), so the status dot must say \\\"broken\\n+## file\\\", not \\\"click Configure\\\".\\n+static func check_status_details(\\n+\\tclient: McpClient,\\n+\\tserver_name: String,\\n+\\tserver_url: String,\\n+\\tlaunch: Dictionary = {},\\n+\\tproject_roots: PackedStringArray = PackedStringArray(),\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tif _uses_merge_tiers(client):\\n+\\t\\treturn _check_status_merged(client, server_name, server_url, launch, project_roots)\\n+\\tvar resolution := client.resolved_config_path_details()\\n+\\tvar path := str(resolution.get(\\\"path\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tvar path_error := str(resolution.get(\\\"error\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tif not path_error.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.ERROR, \\\"error_msg\\\": path_error}\\n+\\tif path.is_empty() or not FileAccess.file_exists(path):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED, \\\"error_msg\\\": \\\"\\\"}\\n+\\tvar read := _read_or_init(path, _status_allows_comments(client))\\n+\\tif not read[\\\"ok\\\"]:\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": McpClient.Status.ERROR, \\\"error_msg\\\": String(read[\\\"error\\\"])}\\n+\\tvar config: Dictionary = read[\\\"data\\\"]\\n+\\tvar holder := _walk_path(config, select_server_key_path(config, client))\\n+\\tif not (holder is Dictionary) or not holder.has(server_name):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\":
