-0,0 +1,448 @@\\n+@tool\\n+class_name McpPathTemplate\\n+extends RefCounted\\n+\\n+## Expands ~ / $HOME / $APPDATA / $XDG_CONFIG_HOME / $LOCALAPPDATA / $USERPROFILE\\n+## inside path templates so per-client descriptors can declare paths declaratively\\n+## without hand-rolling per-OS lookups.\\n+\\n+## #691: dock worker threads (client-status refresh, configure/remove\\n+## actions) and the #678 startup walk's discovery worker expand these\\n+## templates off the main thread, while the spawn step mutates the\\n+## process-global environment around `OS.create_process`\\n+## (`GODOT_AI_OWNER_PID`, `GODOT_AI_PLUGIN_SPAWNED`, `PYTHONPATH`,\\n+## `GODOT_AI_DISABLE_TELEMETRY`). A glibc `getenv` racing a concurrent\\n+## `setenv` can return a freed pointer — rare but process-fatal. All env\\n+## reads in this layer therefore go through `env_lookup`: on the MAIN\\n+## thread it reads live and refreshes a mutex-guarded snapshot; off the\\n+## main thread it serves from the snapshot, so no `OS.get_environment`\\n+## runs concurrently with the spawn window's mutations. Callers pre-warm\\n+## every var their workers can touch via `warm_env_snapshot` (plugin\\n+## `_enter_tree` and the dock's phase-1 refresh prep, both main-thread,\\n+## both before any worker starts).\\n+static var _env_snapshot := {}\\n+static var _env_snapshot_mutex := Mutex.new()\\n+\\n+const LinuxProc := preload(\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/utils/linux_proc.gd\\\")\\n+const _MOUNTINFO_PATH := \\\"/proc/self/mountinfo\\\"\\n+\\n+## `/.flatpak-info` as Flatpak wrote it before this process started, or \\\"\\\" when\\n+## this is not a Flatpak sandbox. The sandbox it describes cannot change while\\n+## the process runs, so one read serves every thread. Guarded by\\n+## `_env_snapshot_mutex`, like the snapshot it is consulted alongside.\\n+static var _flatpak_info := \\\"\\\"\\n+static var _flatpak_info_read := false\\n+## What that sandbox mounted, from `/proc/self/mountinfo`: read with the file\\n+## above, and only inside a sandbox. Empty when it could not be read.\\n+static var _flatpak_mounts: Array[Dictionary] = []\\n+\\n+## Every var this layer and its sibling consumers (`_base.gd`\\n+## `config_file_override_details`, `config_home_override_details`, `_cli_finder.gd` lookups,\\n+## `client_configurator.gd` mode/trace reads) can touch off-main.\\n+## Descriptor-declared config-file/config-home env names are passed as extras\\n+## by the warm callers.\\n+const _BASE_ENV_VARS: Array[String] = [\\n+\\t\\\"HOME\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"USERPROFILE\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\\",\\n+\\t## What `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME` reads in a Flatpak editor — see `_env_source`.\\n+\\t\\\"HOST_XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\\",\\n+\\t## Read by `flatpak_hides_credentials` on the workers that run Configure.\\n+\\t\\\"GODOT_AI_CAPABILITY_DIR\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"APPDATA\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"LOCALAPPDATA\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"SHELL\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"ProgramFiles\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"GODOT_AI_MODE\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"GODOT_AI_STARTUP_TRACE\\\",\\n+\\t## #804 (#752 adoption): _find_venv_python reads this via env_lookup on\\n+\\t## the dock's worker path; without pre-warming, a set override reads as\\n+\\t## empty there and is silently ignored — the exact misconfiguration the\\n+\\t## push_warning in client_configurator.gd exists to surface.\\n+\\t\\\"GODOT_AI_VENV_PYTHON\\\",\\n+]\\n+\\n+\\n+## Thread-safe env read (#691). Main thread: live read + snapshot refresh.\\n+## Worker thread: snapshot only, so it can never race a main-thread\\n+## setenv/unsetenv. A worker read of a never-warmed var returns \\\"\\\" — the\\n+## same value an unset var reads as — never a live OS.get_environment,\\n+## which would reintroduce the race for exactly the vars nobody thought\\n+## to warm. Missing warm-up degrades resolution; it must not touch the\\n+## process-global environment off-main.\\n+static func env_lookup(name: String) -> String:\\n+\\tif OS.get_thread_caller_id() == OS.get_main_thread_id():\\n+\\t\\tvar live := OS.get_environment(name)\\n+\\t\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.lock()\\n+\\t\\t_env_snapshot[name] = live\\n+\\t\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.unlock()\\n+\\t\\treturn live\\n+\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.lock()\\n+\\tvar cached: Variant = _env_snapshot.get(name, null)\\n+\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.unlock()\\n+\\tif cached != null:\\n+\\t\\treturn str(cached)\\n+\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+## Main-thread pre-warm so subsequent worker reads never touch the real\\n+## environment. Idempotent; safe to call before every worker dispatch.\\n+static func warm_env_snapshot(extra_vars: PackedStringArray = PackedStringArray()) -> void:\\n+\\tfor var_name in _BASE_ENV_VARS:\\n+\\t\\tenv_lookup(var_name)\\n+\\tfor var_name in extra_vars:\\n+\\t\\tif not String(var_name).is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\tenv_lookup(String(var_name))\\n+\\t_flatpak_info_text()\\n+\\n+\\n+## Cached `/.flatpak-info`, read on first use from whichever thread asks. Only\\n+## Linux has Flatpak; elsewhere the leading slash would name a drive root.\\n+static func _flatpak_info_text() -> String:\\n+\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.lock()\\n+\\t_read_flatpak_sandbox()\\n+\\tvar info := _flatpak_info\\n+\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.unlock()\\n+\\treturn info\\n+\\n+\\n+## The sandbox's mounts, cached with the description above. The list is built\\n+## once and never changed, so callers on any thread may read it.\\n+static func _flatpak_mount_list() -> Array[Dictionary]:\\n+\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.lock()\\n+\\t_read_flatpak_sandbox()\\n+\\tvar mounts := _flatpak_mounts\\n+\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.unlock()\\n+\\treturn mounts\\n+\\n+\\n+## Caller holds `_env_snapshot_mutex`.\\n+static func _read_flatpak_sandbox() -> void:\\n+\\tif _flatpak_info_read:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_flatpak_info = McpTransportCapability._flatpak_info() if _os_key() == \\\"linux\\\" else \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tvar mountinfo := \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tif not _flatpak_info.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tmountinfo = str(LinuxProc.read_text(_MOUNTINFO_PATH).text)\\n+\\t_flatpak_mounts = McpTransportCapability.parse_mountinfo(mountinfo)\\n+\\t_flatpak_info_read = true\\n+\\n+\\n+## Stand in for the sandbox description so tests can cover Flatpak resolution\\n+## on any host, with `mountinfo` as its `/proc/self/mountinfo`: by default no\\n+## mount evidence at all, never the real file's. Pass null to forget both; the\\n+## next read then asks the real files.\\n+static func _set_flatpak_info_for_test(info: Variant, mountinfo: String = \\\"\\\") -> void:\\n+\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.lock()\\n+\\t_flatpak_info = \\\"\\\" if info == null else str(info)\\n+\\t_flatpak_mounts = McpTransportCapability.parse_mountinfo(mountinfo)\\n+\\t_flatpak_info_read = info != null\\n+\\t_env_snapshot_mutex.unlock()\\n+\\n+\\n+## This editor's Flatpak application ID, or \\\"\\\" outside Flatpak.\\n+static func flatpak_app_id() -> String:\\n+\\treturn McpTransportCapability.flatpak_application_id(_flatpak_info_text())\\n+\\n+\\n+## The host's config directory when this editor is a Flatpak that shares it\\n+## (through the home directory, or a grant for the whole of `xdg-config`); \\\"\\\"\\n+## anywhere else, and when it cannot be resolved. For callers whose own\\n+## default is `OS.get_config_dir()`, which in that sandbox is the per-app\\n+## directory that nothing outside it reads.\\n+static func flatpak_host_config_home() -> String:\\n+\\tif not McpTransportCapability.flatpak_shares_config_home(_flatpak_info_text()):\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tvar config_home := expand(\\\"$XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\\")\\n+\\treturn config_home if config_home.is_absolute_path() else \\\"\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+## Whether this editor's Flatpak sandbox keeps Godot AI's credentials where a\\n+## client outside it does not look. The bridge a client starts reads the\\n+## host's `<config home>/godot-ai/capabilities`, so an entry Configure writes\\n+## from such a sandbox cannot connect, however right its file is.\\n+##\\n+## The server publishes in the host's config directory when the sandbox\\n+## shares it (`McpTransportCapability.linux_config_home_variable`), and\\n+## otherwise in the per-app one, which is the host's only where an\\n+## `xdg-config/` grant mounted the host's directory inside it. Either way the\\n+## host's directory has to be shared read-write, which `flatpak_write_block`\\n+## decides. An editor given `GODOT_AI_CAPABILITY_DIR` publishes somewhere this\\n+## cannot judge, and its client needs the same variable: never hidden here.\\n+static func flatpak_hides_credentials() -> bool:\\n+\\tvar info := _flatpak_info_text()\\n+\\tif info.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tif not env_lookup(McpTransportCapability.CAPABILITY_DIR_ENV).strip_edges().is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tvar config_home := expand(\\\"$XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\\")\\n+\\tif not config_home.is_absolute_path():\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tvar host_directory := config_home.path_join(\\\"godot-ai/capabilities\\\")\\n+\\tif not flatpak_write_block(host_directory.path_join(\\\"http.json\\\")).is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn true\\n+\\tif McpTransportCapability.flatpak_shares_config_home(info):\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tvar grant := McpTransportCapability.flatpak_grant_over(\\n+\\t\\tinfo, host_directory, _home(), config_home\\n+\\t)\\n+\\treturn not str(grant.get(\\\"location\\\", \\\"\\\")).begins_with(\\\"xdg-config/\\\")\\n+\\n+\\n+## The `~/.var/app/<id>` directory of another Flatpak app that `path` lies in,\\n+## when this editor's own Flatpak sandbox does not show it; \\\"\\\" otherwise.\\n+## Flatpak mounts only the running app's directory under `~/.var/app`, and a\\n+## `host` or `home` grant does not widen that. From in here another app's\\n+## settings are therefore unknown rather than absent until the user grants\\n+## that directory by name, and anything created below it would land on the\\n+## sandbox's private tmpfs.\\n+static func hidden_flatpak_app_dir(path: String) -> String:\\n+\\tif _flatpak_info_text().is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tvar home := _home()\\n+\\tif home.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tvar apps := home.path_join(\\\".var/app\\\") + \\\"/\\\"\\n+\\tif not path.begins_with(apps):\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tvar app_dir := apps + path.trim_prefix(apps).get_slice(\\\"/\\\", 0)\\n+\\treturn \\\"\\\" if DirAccess.dir_exists_absolute(app_dir) else app_dir\\n+\\n+\\n+## Pick the right entry from a {\\\"darwin\\\": ..., \\\"windows\\\": ..., \\\"linux\\\": ...} map.\\n+static func resolve(template_map: Dictionary) -> String:\\n+\\tvar key := platform_key(template_map)\\n+\\tif key.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tvar template: String = template_map[key]\\n+\\treturn expand(template)\\n+\\n+\\n+## Return the platform-specific key present in a descriptor map. `unix` is a\\n+## shorthand for macOS and Linux. Public so descriptors can use the same\\n+## platform selection for ordered path-candidate arrays as for one path.\\n+static func platform_key(template_map: Dictionary) -> String:\\n+\\tvar key := _os_key()\\n+\\tif template_map.has(key):\\n+\\t\\treturn key\\n+\\tif (key == \\\"darwin\\\" or key == \\\"linux\\\") and template_map.has(\\\"unix\\\"):\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"unix\\\"\\n+\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+## Expand one path template into zero or more concrete paths. A single `*` is\\n+## allowed inside one DIRECTORY segment (for example `Packages/Claude_*`). The\\n+## wildcard is resolved by enumerating that segment's parent; the remaining\\n+## suffix may name a file that does not exist yet, which lets callers derive a\\n+## deterministic create target for a fresh packaged-app install.\\n+##\\n+## Multiple wildcards fail closed and return no candidates. A wildcard final\\n+## segment may identify an installation directory for `detect_paths`. Returned\\n+## paths are sorted for deterministic tests and diagnostics; callers still\\n+## reject ambiguous config groups rather than picking one.\\n+static func expand_path_candidates(template: String) -> PackedStringArray:\\n+\\tvar expanded := expand(template)\\n+\\tif expanded.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn PackedStringArray()\\n+\\tvar star := expanded.find(\\\"*\\\")\\n+\\tif star < 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn PackedStringArray([expanded])\\n+\\tif expanded.find(\\\"*\\\", star + 1) >= 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn PackedStringArray()\\n+\\n+\\tvar slash_before := maxi(expanded.rfind(\\\"/\\\", star), expanded.rfind(\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\", star))\\n+\\tvar forward_after := expanded.find(\\\"/\\\", star)\\n+\\tvar backward_after := expanded.find(\\\"\\\\\\\\\\\", star)\\n+\\tvar slash_after := forward_after\\n+\\tif slash_after < 0 or (backward_after >= 0 and backward_after < slash_after):\\n+\\t\\tslash_after = backward_after\\n+\\tif slash_before < 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn PackedStringArray()\\n+\\n+\\tvar parent := expanded.substr(0, slash_before)\\n+\\tvar pattern := (\\n+\\t\\texpanded.substr(slash_before + 1)\\n+\\t\\tif slash_after < 0\\n+\\t\\telse expanded.substr(slash_before + 1, slash_after - slash_before - 1)\\n+\\t)\\n+\\tvar suffix := \\\"\\\" if slash_after < 0 else expanded.substr(slash_after + 1)\\n+\\tvar pattern_star := pattern.find(\\\"*\\\")\\n+\\tif pattern_star < 0:\\n+\\t\\treturn PackedStringArray()\\n+\\tvar prefix := pattern.substr(0, pattern_star)\\n+\\tvar ending := pattern.substr(pattern_star + 1)\\n+\\tvar dir := DirAccess.open(parent)\\n+\\tif dir == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn PackedStringArray()\\n+\\n+\\tvar matches := PackedStringArray()\\n+\\tfor child in dir.get_directories():\\n+\\t\\tif _wildcard_segment_matches(String(child), prefix, ending):\\n+\\t\\t\\tvar matched_path := parent.path_join(String(child))\\n+\\t\\t\\tmatches.append(matched_path if suffix.is_empty() else matched_path.path_join(suffix))\\n+\\tmatches.sort()\\n+\\treturn matches\\n+\\n+\\n+## Substitute env vars and ~ in a single template string.\\n+##\\n+## A token that cannot be resolved — the var is unset and no home-derived\\n+## fallback applies, or a dock worker's env snapshot was never warmed with it —\\n+## is LEFT IN PLACE rather than replaced with \\\"\\\". Substituting \\\"\\\" is what turns\\n+## `$USERPROFILE/godot` into `/godot`: a root-relative path that\\n+## `is_absolute_path()` accepts, so every fail-closed guard downstream waves it\\n+## through and the caller reads or writes a directory the user never named.\\n+## `$USERPROFILE` is the easy repro (unset off Windows, and the only one of\\n+## these vars with no fallback), but `$HOME`/`$XDG_CONFIG_HOME`/`$APPDATA`/\\n+## `$LOCALAPPDATA` reach the same state whenever `_home()` is empty, which the\\n+## missing-warm-up degradation above makes reachable on a worker thread.\\n+##\\n+## The surviving token keeps the result non-absolute, so those guards catch it,\\n+## and the unexpanded path they report still shows what failed to resolve.\\n+## `~` is left alone for the same reason: collapsing `~/godot` to the relative\\n+## `godot` aims it at the editor's own working directory.\\n+static func expand(template: String) -> String:\\n+\\tif template.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tvar out := template\\n+\\tif out.begins_with(\\\"~/\\\") or out == \\\"~\\\":\\n+\\t\\tvar home := _home()\\n+\\t\\tif not home.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\tout = home if out == \\\"~\\\" else home.path_join(out.substr(2))\\n+\\t# $HOME, $APPDATA, $LOCALAPPDATA, $USERPROFILE, $XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\n+\\tfor var_name in [\\\"XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\\", \\\"LOCALAPPDATA\\\", \\\"USERPROFILE\\\", \\\"APPDATA\\\", \\\"HOME\\\"]:\\n+\\t\\tvar token := \\\"$%s\\\" % var_name\\n+\\t\\tif out.find(token) < 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tvar value := env_lookup(_env_source(var_name))\\n+\\t\\tif value.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\tvalue = _home_fallback(var_name)\\n+\\t\\tif value.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tout = out.replace(token, value)\\n+\\treturn out\\n+\\n+\\n+## The environment variable a token's value is read from. Flatpak points\\n+## `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` at the editor's own `~/.var/app/<id>/config`, where no\\n+## client looks: a path expanded from it is written, read back, and reported\\n+## as configured without the client ever seeing it. Inside a sandbox the token\\n+## therefore reads the host's variable, so a template always names the file\\n+## the client reads. Whether this sandbox can reach that file is a separate\\n+## question, answered by `flatpak_write_block`. Every grant that shares part\\n+## of the host's config directory mounts it at the host's path, an\\n+## `xdg-config/<dir>` grant included, so no path has to go through the per-app\\n+## directory, where a leftover from an earlier run would pass for the client's\\n+## own file.\\n+static func _env_source(var_name: String) -> String:\\n+\\tif var_name == \\\"XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\\" and not _flatpak_info_text().is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"HOST_XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\\"\\n+\\treturn var_name\\n+\\n+\\n+## Why a file this editor writes at `path` would not be the one a client\\n+## outside its Flatpak sandbox reads there. {} when it would be, and always\\n+## outside Flatpak. Otherwise one of:\\n+## - {\\\"needs\\\": grant}: no read-write grant covers the file's directory, and\\n+##   this is the `--filesystem=` value that would. That is the read-only grant\\n+##   that does cover it when there is one, because a wider grant does not\\n+##   lift it.\\n+## - {\\\"unmounted\\\": directory}: a read-write grant names this directory, but\\n+##   Flatpak mounted nothing there, which is what it does when the directory\\n+##   does not exist as the app starts.\\n+## - {\\\"hidden\\\": directory}: inside a granted directory, this one is an empty\\n+##   tmpfs, which is how Flatpak carries out a denial (`--nofilesystem`).\\n+## - {\\\"read_only\\\": directory}: granted read-write, mounted read-only.\\n+##\\n+## The directory is what has to be shared, not the file: `McpAtomicWrite`\\n+## stages a temporary file beside the target and renames it into place, which\\n+## cannot replace a file Flatpak mounted on its own. It also follows a\\n+## symlinked config file to its target, so that is the file tested.\\n+static func flatpak_write_block(path: String) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar info := _flatpak_info_text()\\n+\\tif info.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {}\\n+\\tvar directory := McpAtomicWrite._resolve_symlink_target(path).get_base_dir()\\n+\\tvar home := _home()\\n+\\tvar inside_home := (\\n+\\t\\tnot home.is_empty() and McpTransportCapability.path_is_within(home, directory)\\n+\\t)\\n+\\tvar grant := McpTransportCapability.flatpak_grant_over(\\n+\\t\\tinfo, directory, home, expand(\\\"$XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\\")\\n+\\t)\\n+\\tif not grant.get(\\\"writable\\\", false):\\n+\\t\\tif not grant.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"needs\\\": str(grant[\\\"location\\\"])}\\n+\\t\\t## Nothing under ~/.var/app comes with the home, so there the app's\\n+\\t\\t## directory has to be granted by name.\\n+\\t\\tvar apps := home.path_join(\\\".var/app\\\") + \\\"/\\\"\\n+\\t\\tif inside_home and directory.begins_with(apps):\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"needs\\\": apps + directory.trim_prefix(apps).get_slice(\\\"/\\\", 0)}\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"needs\\\": \\\"home\\\" if inside_home else directory}\\n+\\tvar root := str(grant[\\\"root\\\"])\\n+\\tvar real_root := McpTransportCapability.real_path(root)\\n+\\tvar mount := McpTransportCapability.flatpak_blocking_mount(\\n+\\t\\t_flatpak_mount_list(),\\n+\\t\\tMcpTransportCapability.real_path(directory),\\n+\\t\\tMcpTransportCapability.real_path(home) if inside_home else real_root,\\n+\\t)\\n+\\tif mount.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {}\\n+\\tvar mount_point := str(mount[\\\"mount_point\\\"])\\n+\\tif mount[\\\"read_only\\\"]:\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"read_only\\\": mount_point}\\n+\\t## A tmpfs above the granted directory is not there because of the grant:\\n+\\t## the grant's own mount is what is missing.\\n+\\tif not McpTransportCapability.path_is_within(real_root, mount_point):\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"unmounted\\\": root}\\n+\\treturn {\\\"hidden\\\": mount_point}\\n+\\n+\\n+## Home-derived default for a var the environment does not define. Returns \\\"\\\"\\n+## when home itself is unresolvable so `expand` leaves the token alone: rooting\\n+## these at an empty home yields a RELATIVE path, not an absent one —\\n+## `\\\"\\\".path_join(\\\".config\\\")` is `.config`, which reads against the editor's\\n+## working directory rather than the user's config dir. `USERPROFILE` has no\\n+## fallback (it is the thing `_home()` itself falls back to).\\n+static func _home_fallback(var_name: String) -> String:\\n+\\tvar home := _home()\\n+\\tif home.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\tmatch var_name:\\n+\\t\\t\\\"XDG_CONFIG_HOME\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn home.path_join(\\\".config\\\")\\n+\\t\\t\\\"APPDATA\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn home.path_join(\\\"AppData/Roaming\\\")\\n+\\t\\t\\\"LOCALAPPDATA\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn home.path_join(\\\"AppData/Local\\\")\\n+\\t\\t\\\"HOME\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn home\\n+\\treturn \\\"\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _os_key() -> String:\\n+\\tmatch OS.get_name():\\n+\\t\\t\\\"macOS\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn \\\"darwin\\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\\"Windows\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn \\\"windows\\\"\\n+\\t\\t_:\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn \\\"linux\\\"\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _wildcard_segment_matches(value: String, prefix: String, ending: String) -> bool:\\n+\\t# Prefix/suffix tests alone allow the two fixed portions to overlap inside a\\n+\\t# too-short value. Glob semantics require room for both portions even when\\n+\\t# `*` matches an empty string.\\n+\\tif value.length() < prefix.length() + ending.length():\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\tif OS.get_name() == \\\"Windows\\\":\\n+\\t\\treturn value.to_lower().begins_with(prefix.to_lower()) and value.to_lower().ends_with(ending.to_lower())\\n+\\treturn value.begins_with(prefix) and value.ends_with(ending)\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _home() -> String:\\n+\\tvar h := env_lookup(\\\"HOME\\\")\\n+\\tif h.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\th = env_lookup(\\\"USERPROFILE\\\")\\n+\\treturn h\"},{\"sha\":\"f2403d77f5b91b1872d37de389b7cbb544a66f16\",\"filename\":\"addons/godot_ai/clients/_path_template.gd.uid\",\"status\":\"added\",\"additions\":1,\"deletions\":0,\"changes\":1,\"blob_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/blob/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_path_template.gd.uid\",\"raw_url\":\"https://github.com/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/raw/3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_path_template.gd.uid\",\"contents_url\":\"https://api.github.com/repos/joaogldc/hackathon_lepv/contents/addons%2Fgodot_ai%2Fclients%2F_path_template.gd.uid?ref=3adb7a77d5b2472908fa911b8a30b28a0a451318\",\"patch\":\"@@ -0,0 +1,158 @@\\n+@tool\\n+class_name McpClientRegistry\\n+extends RefCounted\\n+\\n+## Central enumeration of every supported MCP client. Adding a new client\\n+## means: drop a file in clients/, then append one path below.\\n+##\\n+## Paths, not preloads (#736): a preload array pulled all client descriptor\\n+## scripts into the boot-time compile closure of everything that preloads\\n+## this registry (plugin.gd via client_configurator.gd and mcp_dock.gd),\\n+## stalling \\\"Initializing plugins\\\" on every editor boot. Descriptors are\\n+## only needed when the dock refreshes client statuses or a client_*\\n+## command runs, so they load lazily on first registry access.\\n+\\n+const _CLIENT_SCRIPT_PATHS := [\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/claude_code.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/claude_desktop.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/codex.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/codebuddy.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/grok.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/antigravity.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/cursor.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/windsurf.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/vscode.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/vscode_insiders.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/zed.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/gemini_cli.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/cline.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/kilo_code.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/roo_code.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/zoo_code.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/kiro.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/trae.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/opencode.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/qwen_code.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/kimi_code.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/hermes.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/pi.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/deepseek_harness.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/zcode.gd\\\",\\n+\\t\\\"res://addons/godot_ai/clients/omp.gd\\\",\\n+]\\n+\\n+static var _instances: Array[McpClient] = []\\n+static var _by_id: Dictionary = {}\\n+## First registry access can come from the dock's client-status refresh\\n+## worker thread while the main thread hits it via a client_* command —\\n+## serialize the one-time load so a racing thread can never observe a\\n+## half-built registry. load() itself is thread-safe via ResourceLoader.\\n+static var _load_mutex := Mutex.new()\\n+## True when even a fresh rebuild yields instances missing base-schema\\n+## fields — the deep stale-script state after an in-session self-update\\n+## (#850; docs/releasing.md release-shape rules). Only an editor restart\\n+## heals it; callers surface RESTART_TO_FINISH_UPDATE instead of erroring\\n+## per client. Never reset within a session: rebuilding again cannot help,\\n+## it would only repeat the load work and warning on every dock sweep.\\n+static var _stale_session := false\\n+\\n+const RESTART_TO_FINISH_UPDATE := (\\n+\\t\\\"Godot AI was updated in this editor session. Restart the editor to finish the update.\\\"\\n+)\\n+\\n+\\n+static func all() -> Array[McpClient]:\\n+\\t_ensure_loaded()\\n+\\treturn _instances\\n+\\n+\\n+static func get_by_id(id: String) -> McpClient:\\n+\\t_ensure_loaded()\\n+\\treturn _by_id.get(id, null)\\n+\\n+\\n+static func ids() -> PackedStringArray:\\n+\\tvar out := PackedStringArray()\\n+\\tfor c in all():\\n+\\t\\tout.append(c.id)\\n+\\treturn out\\n+\\n+\\n+static func has_id(id: String) -> bool:\\n+\\t_ensure_loaded()\\n+\\treturn _by_id.has(id)\\n+\\n+\\n+## True when this editor session is running a self-update whose script\\n+## reloads left descriptor state unusable. Client operations short-circuit\\n+## with RESTART_TO_FINISH_UPDATE rather than spamming per-field errors.\\n+static func stale_session_detected() -> bool:\\n+\\t_ensure_loaded()\\n+\\treturn _stale_session\\n+\\n+\\n+## An instance is coherent when fields added to the CURRENT McpClient schema\\n+## read back with their declared types. After an in-session self-update,\\n+## hot-patched or pre-update instances answer Nil for vars the update added\\n+## (#850: `config_path_candidates` and\\n+## `config_file_env` read as Nil, crashing platform_key / String()). The\\n+## reflected `get()` avoids typed-access errors on such instances.\\n+static func _instance_is_coherent(inst: Object) -> bool:\\n+\\tif inst == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn false\\n+\\treturn (\\n+\\t\\tinst.get(\\\"config_path_candidates\\\") is Dictionary\\n+\\t\\tand inst.get(\\\"config_file_env\\\") is String\\n+\\t\\tand inst.get(\\\"path_template\\\") is Dictionary\\n+\\t\\tand inst.get(\\\"server_key_path_aliases\\\") is Array\\n+\\t\\tand inst.get(\\\"config_merge_path_templates\\\") is Dictionary\\n+\\t\\tand inst.get(\\\"config_merge_project_paths\\\") is PackedStringArray\\n+\\t)\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _cache_is_coherent() -> bool:\\n+\\treturn not _instances.is_empty() and _instance_is_coherent(_instances[0])\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _ensure_loaded() -> void:\\n+\\tif _stale_session:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tif _cache_is_coherent():\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_load_mutex.lock()\\n+\\t## Re-check under the lock: another thread may have rebuilt (or concluded\\n+\\t## staleness) while this one waited.\\n+\\tif not _stale_session and not _cache_is_coherent():\\n+\\t\\t## Covers both the first load and the post-self-update rebuild: this\\n+\\t\\t## registry file can survive an update unchanged, so its statics keep\\n+\\t\\t## serving pre-update instances to freshly reloaded callers. A rebuild\\n+\\t\\t## instantiates from the reloaded descriptor scripts, which repairs\\n+\\t\\t## every case except a stale base Script object itself.\\n+\\t\\t_load()\\n+\\t\\tif not _instances.is_empty() and not _cache_is_coherent():\\n+\\t\\t\\t_stale_session = true\\n+\\t\\t\\tpush_warning(\\\"MCP | %s\\\" % RESTART_TO_FINISH_UPDATE)\\n+\\t_load_mutex.unlock()\\n+\\n+\\n+static func _load() -> void:\\n+\\t## Build into locals and publish whole containers last, so the lock-free\\n+\\t## fast path in _ensure_loaded can never see a partially-filled registry.\\n+\\tvar instances: Array[McpClient] = []\\n+\\tvar by_id: Dictionary = {}\\n+\\tfor path in _CLIENT_SCRIPT_PATHS:\\n+\\t\\tvar script := load(path) as GDScript\\n+\\t\\tif script == null:\\n+\\t\\t\\tpush_warning(\\\"MCP | failed to load client descriptor %s\\\" % path)\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tvar inst: McpClient = script.new()\\n+\\t\\tif EditorDebuggerSession:\\n+\\tfor s in get_sessions():\\n+\\t\\tif s is EditorDebuggerSession and s.is_active():\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn s\\n+\\treturn null\\n+\\n+\\n+func _on_screenshot_response(data: Array) -> void:\\n+\\tif data.size() < 6:\\n+\\t\\tpush_warning(\\\"MCP debugger: malformed screenshot response (expected 6 fields, got %d)\\\" % data.size())\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar request_id: String = data[0]\\n+\\tvar pending = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending == null:\\n+\\t\\t## Timed out or unknown — silently drop.\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\n+\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending.connection\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tvar payload := {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"source\\\": \\\"game\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\\"width\\\": int(data[2]),\\n+\\t\\t\\\"height\\\": int(data[3]),\\n+\\t\\t\\\"original_width\\\": int(data[4]),\\n+\\t\\t\\\"original_height\\\": int(data[5]),\\n+\\t\\t\\\"format\\\": \\\"png\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\\"image_base64\\\": data[1],\\n+\\t}\\n+\\t## #777: game helpers append frames_drawn + a stale flag so a capture\\n+\\t## taken while the game's main loop is frozen (backgrounded window) is\\n+\\t## honestly labeled instead of timing out. Older helpers send six fields\\n+\\t## — leave the keys absent rather than guessing.\\n+\\tif data.size() >= 8:\\n+\\t\\tpayload[\\\"frames_drawn\\\"] = int(data[6])\\n+\\t\\tpayload[\\\"stale_frame\\\"] = bool(data[7])\\n+\\t\\tif bool(data[7]):\\n+\\t\\t\\tpayload[\\\"note\\\"] = (\\\"The game window appears backgrounded or its main loop is \\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t+ \\\"stalled; returning the last rendered frame. Focus the game window and \\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t+ \\\"retry for a current frame.\\\")\\n+\\t## Vision Routing: when enabled, describe the frame through the configured\\n+\\t## vision provider on a\\n+\\t## worker thread and reply with the text description instead of the raw\\n+\\t## image (see vision_routing.gd). The router replies for us in that case.\\n+\\tif vision_routing != null and vision_routing.is_routing_enabled():\\n+\\t\\tif vision_routing.route_game_payload(connection, request_id, {\\\"data\\\": payload}):\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tconnection.send_deferred_response(request_id, {\\\"data\\\": payload})\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] <- mcp:screenshot_response (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _on_screenshot_error(data: Array) -> void:\\n+\\tif data.size() < 2:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar request_id: String = data[0]\\n+\\tvar message: String = data[1]\\n+\\tvar pending = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending.connection\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR, message)\\n+\\n+\\n+## #777: the 8s reply timer fired — the screenshot request reached (or should\\n+## have reached) the game helper and no reply came back. Mirror the eval\\n+## timeout split (#518): a not-live game gets the attributed\\n+## _explain_not_live payload; a live game gets GAME_HELPER_TIMEOUT instead of\\n+## the former opaque INTERNAL_ERROR. With the game side's stalled-loop\\n+## stale-frame fallback, a live game only lands here when it has nothing\\n+## rendered to fall back on, its debugger servicing is itself wedged, or the\\n+## helper died mid-run.\\n+func _on_timeout(request_id: String) -> void:\\n+\\tvar pending = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_pending.erase(request_id)\\n+\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending.connection\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar status := get_game_status(-1, GAME_READY_WAIT_SEC)\\n+\\tvar err: Dictionary\\n+\\tif status.get(\\\"status\\\", \\\"\\\") != \\\"live\\\":\\n+\\t\\terr = _explain_not_live(status, ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR)\\n+\\telse:\\n+\\t\\terr = ErrorCodes.make(ErrorCodes.GAME_HELPER_TIMEOUT,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"The game process did not return a frame. The game window may be backgrounded or its main loop blocked — focus the game window and retry, or use game_command to confirm liveness.\\\")\\n+\\t_send_error_response(connection, request_id, err)\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] !! screenshot timeout (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _send_error(connection: McpConnection, request_id: String, code: String, message: String) -> void:\\n+\\t_send_error_response(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.make(code, message))\\n+\\n+\\n+func _send_error_response(connection: McpConnection, request_id: String, err: Dictionary) -> void:\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tconnection.send_deferred_response(request_id, err)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _clear_pending(request_id: String) -> void:\\n+\\tvar pending: Dictionary = _pending.get(request_id, {})\\n+\\tvar timer: SceneTreeTimer = pending.get(\\\"timer\\\")\\n+\\tvar cb: Callable = pending.get(\\\"timeout_callable\\\", Callable())\\n+\\tif timer != null and timer.timeout.is_connected(cb):\\n+\\t\\ttimer.timeout.disconnect(cb)\\n+\\t## #490: eval requests also carry a compile-grace timer and a runtime probe.\\n+\\tvar grace: SceneTreeTimer = pending.get(\\\"grace_timer\\\")\\n+\\tvar gcb: Callable = pending.get(\\\"grace_callable\\\", Callable())\\n+\\tif grace != null and grace.timeout.is_connected(gcb):\\n+\\t\\tgrace.timeout.disconnect(gcb)\\n+\\tvar probe: SceneTreeTimer = pending.get(\\\"probe_timer\\\")\\n+\\tvar pcb: Callable = pending.get(\\\"probe_callable\\\", Callable())\\n+\\tif probe != null and probe.timeout.is_connected(pcb):\\n+\\t\\tprobe.timeout.disconnect(pcb)\\n+\\t_pending.erase(request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+## --- game_eval: execute arbitrary GDScript in the running game ---\\n+\\n+## Editor-side fallback timer for game_eval. MUST stay above the game-side\\n+## EVAL_TIMEOUT_SEC (8.0) in runtime/game_helper.gd and below the dispatcher's\\n+## game_eval budget (15000 ms) in dispatcher.gd — i.e. game 8s < editor 10s <\\n+## dispatcher 15s. This timer only fires when the game never replies at all;\\n+## _on_eval_timeout then attributes the failure (game not live vs never-acked\\n+## vs started-and-hung, #518). Drop timeout_sec at/below 8s and it pre-empts\\n+## the game's more specific \\\"Eval exceeded 8s\\\" message — see the TIMEOUT\\n+## ORDERING note on EVAL_TIMEOUT_SEC.\\n+##\\n+## #500/#859: the *not-ready* path adds EVAL_READY_WAIT_SEC (3s) and the\\n+## EVAL_LIVENESS_WAIT_SEC probe (0.25s) on top of this 10s backstop. That sum\\n+## (13.25s) must also stay below the dispatcher/server 15s budget, or a\\n+## not-yet-ready game makes the server time out opaquely before the editor's\\n+## actionable error returns — exactly the residual ~15s TimeoutError bucket\\n+## #500 tracked down. The cross-file contract is enforced by\\n+## tests/unit/test_game_eval_timeout_ordering.py.\\n+func request_game_eval(\\n+\\tcode: String,\\n+\\trequest_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection,\\n+\\ttimeout_sec: float = 10.0,\\n+) -> void:\\n+\\tif request_id.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tpush_warning(\\\"MCP debugger: eval request missing request_id\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tvar tree := Engine.get_main_loop() as SceneTree\\n+\\tif tree == null:\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"Editor main loop is not a SceneTree — cannot schedule eval\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tvar run_token := _game_run_token\\n+\\tif is_game_capture_ready():\\n+\\t\\t_probe_then_eval(tree, code, request_id, connection, timeout_sec, run_token)\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] waiting for game_helper hello before eval (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\t_wait_then_eval(tree, code, request_id, connection, timeout_sec, run_token)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _wait_then_eval(\\n+\\ttree: SceneTree,\\n+\\tcode: String,\\n+\\trequest_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection,\\n+\\ttimeout_sec: float,\\n+\\trun_token: int,\\n+) -> void:\\n+\\tvar work := ScriptWork.begin(\\\"game_eval_ready\\\")\\n+\\tawait _settle_eval_ready(tree, code, request_id, connection, timeout_sec, run_token)\\n+\\tScriptWork.finish(work)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _settle_eval_ready(\\n+\\ttree: SceneTree, code: String, request_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection, timeout_sec: float, run_token: int,\\n+) -> void:\\n+\\t## #500: eval uses EVAL_READY_WAIT_SEC (not the 20s GAME_READY_WAIT_SEC) so\\n+\\t## the not-ready path returns its actionable error before the 15s server-side\\n+\\t## command timeout fires an opaque TimeoutError. See EVAL_READY_WAIT_SEC.\\n+\\t_pending[request_id] = {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"kind\\\": \\\"eval_ready_wait\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\\"connection\\\": connection,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"run_token\\\": run_token,\\n+\\t}\\n+\\tvar deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + int(EVAL_READY_WAIT_SEC * 1000.0)\\n+\\t## #645: the leading yield guarantees the dispatcher has registered the\\n+\\t## deferred request before any reply (a same-frame reply is dropped as\\n+\\t## expired); the break check bails out early because a parked game never\\n+\\t## registers its capture.\\n+\\tif _eval_ready_frame_waiter.is_valid():\\n+\\t\\tawait _eval_ready_frame_waiter.call()\\n+\\telse:\\n+\\t\\tawait tree.process_frame\\n+\\twhile (\\n+\\t\\t_pending.has(request_id)\\n+\\t\\tand _is_current_game_run(run_token)\\n+\\t\\tand not is_game_capture_ready()\\n+\\t\\tand not _break_active\\n+\\t\\tand Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline\\n+\\t):\\n+\\t\\tif _eval_ready_frame_waiter.is_valid():\\n+\\t\\t\\tawait _eval_ready_frame_waiter.call()\\n+\\t\\telse:\\n+\\t\\t\\tawait tree.process_frame\\n+\\t## end_game_run() owns the reply once it has cleared this tracked wait.\\n+\\tif not _pending.has(request_id):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tif not _is_current_game_run(run_token):\\n+\\t\\t_fail_eval_not_ready(request_id,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"The game run changed before game_eval could start — the game stopped or restarted. Retry against the current run.\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tif not is_game_capture_ready():\\n+\\t\\t## #518: EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY (not INTERNAL_ERROR) — the play session is up\\n+\\t\\t## but the game-side capture didn't register within the short wait. Fast\\n+\\t\\t## and caller-actionable; classifying it apart from the opaque 10s hang\\n+\\t\\t## keeps the INTERNAL_ERROR telemetry bucket meaning \\\"the eval truly hung\\\".\\n+\\t\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\t\\t_send_error_response(connection, request_id,\\n+\\t\\t\\t_explain_not_live(get_game_status(-1, EVAL_READY_WAIT_SEC), ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY))\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\t_probe_then_eval(tree, code, request_id, connection, timeout_sec, run_token)\\n+\\n+\\n+## #859: registration is run-scoped but not a liveness guarantee. Ping the\\n+## helper's debugger capture and let it report whether its _process beacon is\\n+## still advancing before arming the much longer eval backstop.\\n+func _probe_then_eval(\\n+\\ttree: SceneTree,\\n+\\tcode: String,\\n+\\trequest_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection,\\n+\\ttimeout_sec: float,\\n+\\trun_token: int,\\n+) -> void:\\n+\\tif not _is_current_game_run(run_token) or not is_game_capture_ready():\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"The game run changed before game_eval could be checked — the game stopped or restarted. Retry against the current run.\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar session: EditorDebuggerSession = _first_active_session()\\n+\\tif session == null:\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"Game-side capture registered but its debugger session is no longer active — the game likely just stopped or is restarting. Confirm it is running and retry.\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tvar timer := tree.create_timer(EVAL_LIVENESS_WAIT_SEC)\\n+\\tvar timeout_callable := func() -> void: _on_eval_liveness_timeout(request_id)\\n+\\ttimer.timeout.connect(timeout_callable)\\n+\\t_pending[request_id] = {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"kind\\\": \\\"eval_liveness\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\\"connection\\\": connection,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"timer\\\": timer,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"timeout_callable\\\": timeout_callable,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"code\\\": code,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"eval_timeout_sec\\\": timeout_sec,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"run_token\\\": run_token,\\n+\\t}\\n+\\tsession.send_message(\\\"mcp:eval_liveness\\\", [request_id])\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] -> mcp:eval_liveness (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _on_eval_liveness_timeout(request_id: String) -> void:\\n+\\tvar pending_entry = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending_entry == null or str(pending_entry.get(\\\"kind\\\", \\\"\\\")) != \\\"eval_liveness\\\":\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending_entry.get(\\\"connection\\\")\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY,\\n+\\t\\t(\\\"The game helper did not answer the liveness probe within %.0fms — the game may be backgrounded, frozen, stopped, or restarting. Focus the game window (or relaunch it) and retry.\\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t% (EVAL_LIVENESS_WAIT_SEC * 1000.0)))\\n+\\n+\\n+func _on_eval_liveness_response(data: Array) -> void:\\n+\\tif data.size() < 2:\\n+\\t\\tpush_warning(\\\"MCP debugger: malformed eval liveness response\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar request_id := str(data[0])\\n+\\tvar pending_entry = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending_entry == null or str(pending_entry.get(\\\"kind\\\", \\\"\\\")) != \\\"eval_liveness\\\":\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending_entry.get(\\\"connection\\\")\\n+\\tvar code := str(pending_entry.get(\\\"code\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\tvar timeout_sec := float(pending_entry.get(\\\"eval_timeout_sec\\\", 10.0))\\n+\\tvar run_token := int(pending_entry.get(\\\"run_token\\\", -1))\\n+\\tvar loop_live := bool(data[1])\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tif not _is_current_game_run(run_token) or not is_game_capture_ready():\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"The game run changed while game_eval liveness was being checked — the game stopped or restarted. Retry against the current run.\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tif not loop_live:\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"The game helper is registered but its main loop is not advancing — the game window may be backgrounded or the game may be frozen. Focus the game window (or relaunch it) and retry.\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar current_tree := Engine.get_main_loop() as SceneTree\\n+\\tif current_tree == null:\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"Editor main loop is not a SceneTree — cannot schedule eval\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_send_eval(current_tree, code, request_id, connection, timeout_sec)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _send_eval(\\n+\\ttree: SceneTree,\\n+\\tcode: String,\\n+\\trequest_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection,\\n+\\ttimeout_sec: float,\\n+) -> void:\\n+\\tvar session: EditorDebuggerSession = _first_active_session()\\n+\\tif session == null:\\n+\\t\\t## #518: capture reported ready but the debugger session is no longer live\\n+\\t\\t## (the game just stopped / is restarting) — a not-ready race, so the same\\n+\\t\\t## caller-actionable EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY rather than the opaque hang bucket.\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"Game-side capture registered but its debugger session is no longer active — the game likely just stopped or is restarting. Confirm it's running and retry.\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tvar timer: SceneTreeTimer = tree.create_timer(timeout_sec)\\n+\\tvar timeout_callable := func() -> void: _on_eval_timeout(request_id, timeout_sec)\\n+\\ttimer.timeout.connect(timeout_callable)\\n+\\n+\\t## #490: arm the compile-grace timer. _on_eval_grace concludes a parse error\\n+\\t## only when the game acked the eval (it received the message and started\\n+\\t## reload()) but never sent mcp:eval_compiled — see there for why a missing\\n+\\t## ack must NOT be read as a compile error.\\n+\\tvar grace: SceneTreeTimer = tree.create_timer(EVAL_COMPILE_GRACE_SEC)\\n+\\tvar grace_callable := func() -> void: _on_eval_grace(request_id)\\n+\\tgrace.timeout.connect(grace_callable)\\n+\\n+\\t_pending[request_id] = {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"kind\\\": \\\"eval\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\\"connection\\\": connection,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"run_token\\\": _game_run_token,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"timer\\\": timer,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"timeout_callable\\\": timeout_callable,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"grace_timer\\\": grace,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"grace_callable\\\": grace_callable,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"acked\\\": false,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"compiled\\\": false,\\n+\\t}\\n+\\n+\\tsession.send_message(\\\"mcp:eval\\\", [request_id, code])\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] -> mcp:eval (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+## #518: the 10s editor-side backstop fired — the game never replied at all.\\n+## Attribute the failure instead of emitting a one-size-fits-all INTERNAL_ERROR:\\n+##\\n+## - game not live anymore (parked in a debugger break, stopped, crashed):\\n+##   the eval couldn't run/finish for a *game-state* reason. Reply with the\\n+##   same caller-actionable EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY + `_explain_not_live` payload\\n+##   the pre-hello break path already uses — a break freezes the game's idle\\n+##   loop, so any awaiting eval parks here even though sync evals still work.\\n+## - game live but never acked the eval: its main thread never serviced the\\n+##   debugger message (long frame/load, CPU-bound prior eval, or a\\n+##   backgrounded window whose idle loop is frozen).\\n+## - game live, acked, compiled: the eval genuinely started and never\\n+##   finished, and the game couldn't even self-report via its own 8s guard\\n+##   (which needs a ticking idle loop) — hung await, CPU-bound loop, or a\\n+##   reply the debugger channel dropped.\\n+##\\n+## The live branches reply EVAL_HUNG: the eval code never finished. That code\\n+## plus the game-side 8s guard (also EVAL_HUNG, via mcp:eval_error's code\\n+## element) empties the former INTERNAL_ERROR bucket on this path.\\n+func _on_eval_timeout(request_id: String, timeout_sec: float) -> void:\\n+\\tvar pending_entry = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending_entry == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar run_token := int(pending_entry.get(\\\"run_token\\\", _game_run_token))\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\tvar conn: McpConnection = pending_entry.connection\\n+\\tif conn == null or not is_instance_valid(conn):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tif run_token != _game_run_token:\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(conn, request_id, ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"The game run changed before game_eval completed — the original game stopped or restarted. Retry against the current run.\\\")\\n+\\t\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] !! eval timeout from prior run (%s, run=%d, current=%d)\\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t% [request_id, run_token, _game_run_token])\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar status := get_game_status(-1, EVAL_READY_WAIT_SEC)\\n+\\tif str(status.get(\\\"status\\\", \\\"\\\")) != \\\"live\\\":\\n+\\t\\t_send_error_response(conn, request_id,\\n+\\t\\t\\t_explain_not_live(status, ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY))\\n+\\t\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] !! eval timeout, game not live (%s, status=%s)\\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t% [request_id, str(status.get(\\\"status\\\", \\\"\\\"))])\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar message: String\\n+\\tif not bool(pending_entry.get(\\\"acked\\\", false)):\\n+\\t\\tmessage = (\\\"Game eval was sent but the game never picked it up within %.0fs — \\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t+ \\\"its main thread is busy or frozen (a long frame/load, a CPU-bound \\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t+ \\\"prior eval, or a backgrounded game window whose loop is throttled). \\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t+ \\\"Check logs_read(source='game') and retry.\\\") % timeout_sec\\n+\\telse:\\n+\\t\\tmessage = (\\\"Game eval compiled and started running but never returned within \\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t+ \\\"%.0fs — the code is likely stuck in an infinite loop or awaiting a \\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t+ \\\"signal/timer that never fires (a backgrounded game window also freezes \\\"\\n+\\t\\t\\t+ \\\"awaits). Check logs_read(source='game').\\\") % timeout_sec\\n+\\t_send_error(conn, request_id, ErrorCodes.EVAL_HUNG, message)\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] !! eval timeout (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _on_eval_response(data: Array) -> void:\\n+\\tif data.size() < 2:\\n+\\t\\tpush_warning(\\\"MCP debugger: malformed eval response (expected 2 fields, got %d)\\\" % data.size())\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar request_id: String = data[0]\\n+\\tvar pending_entry = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending_entry == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\n+\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending_entry.connection\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tvar result_json: String = data[1] if data.size() > 1 else \\\"null\\\"\\n+\\tvar json := JSON.new()\\n+\\tvar parse_err := json.parse(result_json)\\n+\\tconnection.send_deferred_response(request_id, {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"data\\\": {\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"result\\\": json.data if parse_err == OK else result_json,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"source\\\": \\\"game\\\",\\n+\\t\\t}\\n+\\t})\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] <- mcp:eval_response (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+## #518: codes the game side may attach as mcp:eval_error's optional third\\n+## payload element. Allowlisted so a game process can't mint arbitrary\\n+## top-level error codes over the debugger channel; anything else (including\\n+## the legacy two-element payload from an older game helper mid-update)\\n+## falls back to INTERNAL_ERROR exactly as before.\\n+const _GAME_EVAL_ERROR_CODES: Array[String] = [\\n+\\tErrorCodes.EVAL_HUNG,\\n+\\tErrorCodes.EVAL_RESULT_TOO_LARGE,\\n+]\\n+\\n+\\n+func _on_eval_error(data: Array) -> void:\\n+\\tif data.size() < 2:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar request_id: String = data[0]\\n+\\tvar message: String = data[1]\\n+\\tvar pending_entry = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending_entry == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending_entry.connection\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar code := ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR\\n+\\tif data.size() > 2 and str(data[2]) in _GAME_EVAL_ERROR_CODES:\\n+\\t\\tcode = str(data[2])\\n+\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, code, message)\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] <- mcp:eval_error (%s): %s\\\" % [request_id, message])\\n+\\n+\\n+## #490: the game sends this at the top of _handle_eval, BEFORE reload() (so it\\n+## survives a parse-error abort). Check your eval code's syntax.\\\")\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] !! eval compile error (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+## #490: the game sends this the instant reload() of the eval source\\n+## succeeds. Check logs_read(source='game').\\\"\\n+\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.EVAL_RUNTIME_ERROR, msg)\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] <- mcp:eval_runtime_error (%s): %s\\\" % [request_id, message])\\n+\\n+\\n+## #490: arm one probe tick for an in-flight eval. _on_eval_probe_tick(request_id: String) -> void:\\n+\\tif not _pending.has(request_id):\\n+\\t\\treturn  ## resolved — stop probing\\n+\\tvar session: EditorDebuggerSession = _first_active_session()\\n+\\tif session != null and session.is_active():\\n+\\t\\tsession.send_message(\\\"mcp:eval_check\\\", [request_id])\\n+\\t_arm_eval_probe(request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+## --- native embedded Game View runtime control (#939) ---\\n+\\n+static func game_debug_verification(\\n+\\taction: String, state_before: Dictionary, state_after: Dictionary\\n+) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar before_ticks := int(state_before.get(\\\"process_ticks\\\", -1))\\n+\\tvar after_ticks := int(state_after.get(\\\"process_ticks\\\", -1))\\n+\\tvar ticks_advanced := after_ticks - before_ticks if before_ticks >= 0 and after_ticks >= 0 else -1\\n+\\tmatch action:\\n+\\t\\t\\\"suspend\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"verified\\\" if bool(state_after.get(\\\"suspended\\\", false)) \\\"pending\\\", \\\"ticks_advanced\\\": ticks_advanced}\\n+\\t\\t\\\"next_frame\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\tif before_ticks < 0 or after_ticks < 0:\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"multiple_process_ticks\\\", \\\"ticks_advanced\\\": ticks_advanced}\\n+\\t\\t\\tif ticks_advanced == 1 and bool(state_after.get(\\\"suspended\\\", false)):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"verified\\\", \\\"ticks_advanced\\\": 1}\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"pending\\\", \\\"ticks_advanced\\\": ticks_advanced}\\n+\\treturn {\\\"status\\\": \\\"failed\\\", \\\"reason\\\": \\\"unsupported_action\\\", \\\"ticks_advanced\\\": ticks_advanced}\\n+\\n+\\n+func _active_game_debug_mutation() -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tfor raw_request_id in _pending.keys():\\n+\\t\\tvar := str(pending_entry.get(\\\"action\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n+\\t\\tif active_action in [\\\"suspend\\\", \\\"resume\\\", \\\"next_frame\\\"]:\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"request_id\\\": str(raw_request_id), \\\"action\\\": active_action}\\n+\\treturn {}\\n+\\n+\\n+func request_game_debug_control(\\n+\\taction: String,\\n+\\trequest_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection,\\n+\\ttimeout_sec: float = \\\"debug_status\\\"]:\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.VALUE_OUT_OF_RANGE,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"Unsupported game debug action: %s\\\" % action)\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar tree := state_before: Dictionary = pending_entry.get(\\\"state_before\\\", {})\\n+\\tvar verdict := game_debug_verification(action, state_before, state_after)\\n+\\tmatch str(verdict.get(\\\"status\\\", err := ErrorCodes.make(ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\\"Game debug action '%s' violated its verification contract\\\" % action)\\n+\\t\\t\\terr[\\\"error\\\"][\\\"data\\\"] = {\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\\"action\\\": action,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\\"reason\\\": verdict.get(\\\"reason\\\", \\\"verification_failed\\\"),\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\\"ticks_advanced\\\": verdict.get(\\\"ticks_advanced\\\", -1),\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\\"state_before\\\": state_before,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\\"state_after\\\": state_after,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\\"focus_handoff\\\": pending_entry.get(\\\"focus_handoff\\\", {}),\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\\"path\\\": pending_entry.get(\\\"path\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t\\\"game_view_ui_synced\\\": pending_entry.get(\\\"game_view_ui_synced\\\", false),\\n+\\t\\t\\t}\\n+\\t\\t\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\t\\t\\tif connection != null and is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\t_send_error_response(connection, request_id, err)\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar payload := {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"action\\\": action,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"changed\\\": true,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"verified\\\": true,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"state_before\\\": state_before,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"state_after\\\": state_after,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"focus_handoff\\\": pending_entry.get(\\\"focus_handoff\\\", {}),\\n+\\t\\t\\\"path\\\": pending_entry.get(\\\"path\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n+\\t\\t\\\"game_view_ui_synced\\\": pending_entry.get(\\\"game_view_ui_synced\\\", false),\\n+\\t}\\n+\\tif action == \\\"next_frame\\\":\\n+\\t\\tpayload[\\\"ticks_advanced\\\"] = int(verdict.get(\\\"ticks_advanced\\\", -1))\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\tif connection != null and is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\tconnection.send_deferred_response(request_id, {\\\"data\\\": payload})\\n+\\n+\\n+func _focus_embedded_game_view() -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar result := {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"attempted\\\": true,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"main_screen\\\": \\\"Game\\\",\\n+\\t\\t\\\"embedded_process_found\\\": false,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"focused\\\": false,\\n+\\t}\\n+\\tEditorInterface.set_main_screen_editor(\\\"Game\\\")\\n+\\tvar base := EditorInterface.get_base_control()\\n+\\tif base == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn result\\n+\\tvar embedded_nodes: Array[Node] = []\\n+\\t_collect_nodes_of_class(base, \\\"EmbeddedProcess\\\", embedded_nodes)\\n+\\tfor node in embedded_nodes:\\n+\\t\\tif node is Control:\\n+\\t\\t\\tvar embedded := node as Control\\n+\\t\\t\\tresult[\\\"embedded_process_found\\\"] = true\\n+\\t\\t\\tembedded.grab_focus()\\n+\\t\\t\\tresult[\\\"focused\\\"] = embedded.has_focus()\\n+\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n+\\treturn result\\n+\\n+\\n+static func direct_game_debug_message(action: String, desired_suspended: bool) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tmatch action:\\n+\\t\\t\\\"suspend\\\", \\\"resume\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"message\\\": \\\"scene:suspend_changed\\\", \\\"data\\\": [desired_suspended]}\\n+\\t\\t\\\"next_frame\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"message\\\": \\\"scene:next_frame\\\", \\\"data\\\": []}\\n+\\treturn {}\\n+\\n+\\n+func _emit_game_debug_runtime_action(action: String, desired_suspended: bool) -> Dictionary:\\n+\\tvar embed_action := EMBED_NEXT_FRAME if action == \\\"next_frame\\\" else EMBED_SUSPEND_TOGGLE\\n+\\tvar base := EditorInterface.get_base_control()\\n+\\tif base != null:\\n+\\t\\tvar debuggers: Array[Node] = []\\n+\\t\\t_collect_nodes_of_class(base, \\\"ScriptEditorDebugger\\\", debuggers)\\n+\\t\\tfor debugger in debuggers:\\n+\\t\\t\\tif not debugger.has_signal(\\\"embed_shortcut_requested\\\"):\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\t\\tif debugger.get_signal_connection_list(\\\"embed_shortcut_requested\\\").is_empty():\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\t\\tdebugger.emit_signal(\\\"embed_shortcut_requested\\\", embed_action)\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn {\\\"ok\\\": true, \\\"path\\\": \\\"embed_signal\\\", \\\"game_view_ui_synced\\\": true}\\n+\\n+\\tvar session := _first_active_session()\\n+\\tif session == null or not session.is_active():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"ok\\\": false, \\\"reason\\\": \\\"no_active_debugger_session\\\"}\\n+\\tvar direct := direct_game_debug_message(action, desired_suspended)\\n+\\tif direct.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\treturn {\\\"ok\\\": false, \\\"reason\\\": \\\"unsupported_action\\\"}\\n+\\tsession.send_message(str(direct.message), direct.data as Array)\\n+\\t## The direct debugger-session path works without embedding, but bypasses\\n+\\t## Game View's suspend button, so its visual pressed state is not synchronized.\\n+\\treturn {\\\"ok\\\": true, \\\"path\\\": \\\"direct_session\\\", \\\"game_view_ui_synced\\\": false}\\n+\\n+\\n+func _fail_pending_game_debug_controls_not_ready(message: String) -> void:\\n+\\tfor request_id in _pending.keys():\\n+\\t\\tvar pending_entry: Dictionary = _pending.get(request_id, {})\\n+\\t\\tif str(pending_entry.get(\\\"kind\\\", \\\"\\\")) != \\\"game_debug_control\\\":\\n+\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n+\\t\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending_entry.get(\\\"connection\\\")\\n+\\t\\t_clear_pending(str(request_id))\\n+\\t\\tif connection != null and is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\t\\t_send_error(connection, str(request_id), ErrorCodes.EVAL_GAME_NOT_READY, message)\\n+\\n+\\n+## --- game_command: curated runtime game operations ---\\n+\\n+func request_game_command(\\n+\\top: String,\\n+\\tparams: Dictionary,\\n+\\trequest_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection,\\n+\\ttimeout_sec: float = 10.0,\\n+) -> void:\\n+\\tif request_id.is_empty():\\n+\\t\\tpush_warning(\\\"MCP debugger: game command request missing request_id\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tvar tree := Engine.get_main_loop() as SceneTree\\n+\\tif tree == null:\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"Editor main loop is not a SceneTree — cannot schedule game command\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tif is_game_capture_ready():\\n+\\t\\t_send_game_command(tree, op, params, request_id, connection, timeout_sec)\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] waiting for game_helper hello before game_command (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\t_wait_then_game_command(tree, op, params, request_id, connection, timeout_sec)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _wait_then_game_command(\\n+\\ttree: SceneTree,\\n+\\top: String,\\n+\\tparams: Dictionary,\\n+\\trequest_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection,\\n+\\ttimeout_sec: float,\\n+) -> void:\\n+\\tvar work := ScriptWork.begin(\\\"game_command_ready\\\")\\n+\\tawait _settle_game_command_ready(tree, op, params, request_id, connection, timeout_sec)\\n+\\tScriptWork.finish(work)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _settle_game_command_ready(\\n+\\ttree: SceneTree, op: String, params: Dictionary, request_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection, timeout_sec: float,\\n+) -> void:\\n+\\tvar deadline := Time.get_ticks_msec() + int(GAME_READY_WAIT_SEC * 1000.0)\\n+\\t## #645: the leading yield guarantees the dispatcher has registered the\\n+\\t## deferred request before any reply (a same-frame reply is dropped as\\n+\\t## expired); the break check bails out early because a parked game never\\n+\\t## registers its capture.\\n+\\tawait tree.process_frame\\n+\\twhile not is_game_capture_ready() and not _break_active and Time.get_ticks_msec() < deadline:\\n+\\t\\tawait tree.process_frame\\n+\\tif not is_game_capture_ready():\\n+\\t\\t_send_error_response(connection, request_id,\\n+\\t\\t\\t_explain_not_live(get_game_status(-1, GAME_READY_WAIT_SEC), ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR))\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_send_game_command(tree, op, params, request_id, connection, timeout_sec)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _send_game_command(\\n+\\ttree: SceneTree,\\n+\\top: String,\\n+\\tparams: Dictionary,\\n+\\trequest_id: String,\\n+\\tconnection: McpConnection,\\n+\\ttimeout_sec: float,\\n+) -> void:\\n+\\tvar session: EditorDebuggerSession = _first_active_session()\\n+\\tif session == null:\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"No active debugger session — is the game actually running?\\\")\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tvar timer: SceneTreeTimer = tree.create_timer(timeout_sec)\\n+\\tvar timeout_callable := func() -> void:\\n+\\t\\tvar pending_entry = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\t\\tif pending_entry == null:\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t\\t_pending.erase(request_id)\\n+\\t\\tvar conn: McpConnection = pending_entry.connection\\n+\\t\\tif conn == null or not is_instance_valid(conn):\\n+\\t\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t\\t_send_error(conn, request_id, ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR,\\n+\\t\\t\\t\\\"Game command '%s' timed out after %.0fs\\\" % [op, timeout_sec])\\n+\\t\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] !! game_command timeout (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\ttimer.timeout.connect(timeout_callable)\\n+\\t_pending[request_id] = {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"connection\\\": connection,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"timer\\\": timer,\\n+\\t\\t\\\"timeout_callable\\\": timeout_callable,\\n+\\t}\\n+\\n+\\tsession.send_message(\\\"mcp:game_command\\\", [request_id, op, JSON.stringify(params)])\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] -> mcp:game_command %s (%s)\\\" % [op, request_id])\\n+\\n+\\n+func _on_game_command_response(data: String = data[0]\\n+\\tvar pending_entry = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending_entry == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\n+\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending_entry.connection\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\n+\\tvar result_json: String = data[1] if data.size() > 1 else \\\"{}\\\"\\n+\\tvar json := JSON.new()\\n+\\tvar parse_err := json.parse(result_json)\\n+\\tconnection.send_deferred_response(request_id, {\\n+\\t\\t\\\"data\\\": json.data if parse_err == OK else {\\\"source\\\": \\\"game\\\", \\\"result\\\": result_json}\\n+\\t})\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] <- mcp:game_command_response (%s)\\\" % request_id)\\n+\\n+\\n+func _on_game_command_error(data: Array) -> void:\\n+\\tif data.size() < 2:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\tvar request_id: String = data[0]\\n+\\tvar message: String = data[1]\\n+\\tvar pending_entry = _pending.get(request_id)\\n+\\tif pending_entry == null:\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_clear_pending(request_id)\\n+\\tvar connection: McpConnection = pending_entry.connection\\n+\\tif connection == null or not is_instance_valid(connection):\\n+\\t\\treturn\\n+\\t_send_error(connection, request_id, ErrorCodes.INTERNAL_ERROR, message)\\n+\\tif _log_buffer:\\n+\\t\\t_log_buffer.log(\\\"[debug] <-
